@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_App.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — AppSheet support (stage O6) · 11 Nov 2026
 * Fifth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_App › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App. This file replaces, by name (last definition wins):
 *   onOpen          — the O5 menu + submenu "AppSheet (mobile)"
 *   menuSelfTestAll — core + PDF + panels + O5 + O6
 * Core, PDF, UI and Reports files are unchanged.
 *
 * How the mobile app works (guide: claude/ops/APPSHEET_GUIDE.md):
 *   The AppSheet app adds challans with a temporary number "APP-xxxxxxxx" and lines, then "Submit for posting" sets
 *   Status = Submitted. A timed trigger (every 5 min, Settings AppSweepMinutes) runs appSweep(): it checks the challan,
 *   gives it the real number (CAE/DC/yyyy/nnn), fills From/To, posts it with the same postChallan() as the panel,
 *   copies damage photos to the Damage rows and makes the challan PDF. A challan that cannot be posted goes back to
 *   Draft with the reason in AppMsg, so the storekeeper sees it in the app.
 *   AppSheet only calls standalone scripts, so no AppSheet bot is needed or used — this works on any AppSheet plan.
 * Also: QR labels (items, locations) as a printable PDF, and the AppStock tab (stock per item × location for the app).
 */
var CAE_O6_VERSION = 'O6 · 11 Nov 2026';
var APP_PREFIX_ = 'APP-';
var QR_URL_ = 'https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=300x300&margin=4&data={data}';
var O6_SETTINGS_ = [
  ['AppAutoPost', 'Yes', 'AppSheet challans: Yes = post automatically; No = only number them, the office posts in the panel'],
  ['AppMakePdf', 'Yes', 'Make the challan PDF after an app challan is posted'],
  ['AppSweepMinutes', '5', 'How often app challans are picked up: 1, 5, 10, 15 or 30 minutes'],
  ['AppMaxBackDays', '31', 'App challans dated more than this many days ago are refused (enter old challans in the panel)'],
  ['QrUrl', QR_URL_, 'QR image service for labels; {data} = item or location code'],
];
function isAppNo_(v) { return String(v || '').indexOf(APP_PREFIX_) === 0; }
function colLetter_(n) { var s = ''; while (n > 0) { var m = (n - 1) % 26; s = String.fromCharCode(65 + m) + s; n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26); } return s; }

// ======================================================================= upgrade (menu, run once, safe to repeat)
function upgradeO6(db) {
  var done = [];
  if (db.ensureColumn('Challans', 'SubmittedBy', 'Notes')) done.push('Challans + SubmittedBy');
  if (db.ensureColumn('Challans', 'AppMsg', 'SubmittedBy')) done.push('Challans + AppMsg');
  if (db.ensureColumn('ChallanLines', 'Photo', 'Remarks')) done.push('ChallanLines + Photo');
  O6_SETTINGS_.forEach(function (s) {
    if (find(db.rows('Settings'), 'Key', s[0])) return;
    var row = { Key: s[0], Value: s[1] }; if (db.headers('Settings').indexOf('Notes') >= 0) row.Notes = s[2];
    db.insert('Settings', row); done.push('Settings ' + s[0]);
  });
  if (db.ss) {
    if (ensureListValue_(db, 'PostStatus', 'Submitted', 'Challans', 'Status')) done.push('Lists PostStatus + Submitted');
    if (buildAppStock_(db)) done.push('AppStock tab');
  }
  log_(db, 'Upgrade O6', '', done.join('; ') || 'up to date');
  return done;
}
// Adds a value to a Lists column and points the tab's dropdown at Lists rows 2–20 (strictness kept as it was).
function ensureListValue_(db, list, value, tab, col) {
  var ls = db.sheet('Lists'), h = ls.getRange(1, 1, 1, ls.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String), j = h.indexOf(list);
  if (j < 0) throw new Error('Lists: no column ' + list);
  var vals = ls.getRange(2, j + 1, Math.max(ls.getLastRow() - 1, 1), 1).getValues().map(function (r) { return String(r[0]); }).filter(String);
  var added = vals.indexOf(value) < 0;
  if (added) ls.getRange(vals.length + 2, j + 1).setValue(value);
  var L = colLetter_(j + 1), s = db.sheet(tab), c = db.headers(tab).indexOf(col) + 1;
  if (c < 1) throw new Error(tab + ': no column ' + col);
  var rng = s.getRange(2, c, Math.max(s.getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1), old = s.getRange(2, c).getDataValidation();
  rng.setDataValidation(SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInRange(ls.getRange('$' + L + '$2:$' + L + '$20'), true).setAllowInvalid(old ? old.getAllowInvalid() : false).build());
  return added;
}
// AppStock: one row per item × location with the quantity there now (live formula from StockMoves). Read-only in the app.
function appStockFormula_(db) {
  var c = function (k) { var L = colL(db, 'StockMoves', k); return 'StockMoves!$' + L + '$2:$' + L; };
  var it = c('ItemCode'), q = c('Qty'), fr = c('FromLoc'), to = c('ToLoc');
  return '=IFERROR(QUERY(ARRAYFORMULA({' + it + '&"|"&' + to + ',' + it + ',' + to + ',' + q + '*1;' + it + '&"|"&' + fr + ',' + it + ',' + fr + ',-' + q + '}),' +
    '"select Col1, Col2, Col3, sum(Col4) where Col2 <> \'\' and Col3 <> \'\' group by Col1, Col2, Col3 label sum(Col4) \'\'",0),"")';
}
function buildAppStock_(db) {
  var ss = db.ss, s = ss.getSheetByName('AppStock'), isNew = !s;
  if (!s) s = ss.insertSheet('AppStock', ss.getSheets().length);
  s.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([['StockKey', 'ItemCode', 'LocID', 'Qty']]).setBackground('#17707D').setFontColor('#FFFFFF').setFontWeight('bold');
  s.getRange('A2').setFormula(appStockFormula_(db));
  s.getRange('D2:D').setNumberFormat('0.##');
  s.setFrozenRows(1); s.setTabColor('#17707D');
  if (!s.getProtections(SpreadsheetApp.ProtectionType.SHEET).length) s.protect().setDescription('AppStock — formula for the AppSheet app (O6)').setWarningOnly(true);
  return isNew;
}

// ======================================================================= one app challan
// c = Challans row with Status Submitted. Checks → real number → From/To → post (Settings AppAutoPost) → photos → PDF.
function appChallanDefaults_(db, c, job) {
  var kind = String(c.Kind || ''), sl = kind === 'Transfer' ? '' : siteLoc(db, job.SiteID);
  var d = { Delivery: [LOC.YARD, job.JobType === 'Sale' ? LOC.SOLD : sl], Sale: [LOC.YARD, LOC.SOLD], Return: [sl, LOC.YARD], Transfer: ['', ''] }[kind];
  if (!d) throw new Error('Kind must be Delivery, Return, Transfer or Sale');
  return { FromLoc: blank(c.FromLoc) ? d[0] : String(c.FromLoc), ToLoc: blank(c.ToLoc) ? d[1] : String(c.ToLoc) };
}
function appProcessChallan_(db, c, o) {
  o = o || {};
  var who = c.SubmittedBy || 'app', d = c.ChallanDate;
  if (!(d instanceof Date)) throw new Error('Challan date "' + d + '" was not read as a date. In AppSheet set Data › Challans › Locale = English (United Kingdom), then submit again.');
  var t = dayNum(today()), back = num(S(db, 'AppMaxBackDays', 31)) || 31;
  if (dayNum(d) > t) throw new Error('Challan date ' + fmtDay(dayNum(d)) + ' is in the future');
  if (dayNum(d) < t - back) throw new Error('Challan date ' + fmtDay(dayNum(d)) + ' is more than ' + back + ' days ago — enter old challans in the sheet panel');
  if (blank(c.JobNo)) throw new Error('Choose the job');
  var job = must(db, 'Jobs', 'JobNo', c.JobNo);
  if (job.Status === 'Closed') throw new Error(job.JobNo + ' is Closed');
  var lines = db.rows('ChallanLines').filter(function (l) { return String(l.ChallanNo) === String(c.ChallanNo); });
  if (!lines.length) throw new Error('The challan has no lines');
  var loc = appChallanDefaults_(db, c, job);
  if (!loc.FromLoc || !loc.ToLoc) throw new Error('Transfer: choose From and To locations');
  if (loc.FromLoc === loc.ToLoc) throw new Error('From and To are the same location');
  // numbering (only once: a challan already numbered keeps its number when it is submitted again)
  var no = String(c.ChallanNo), patch = { FromLoc: loc.FromLoc, ToLoc: loc.ToLoc, Status: 'Draft', AppMsg: '' };
  if (isAppNo_(no)) {
    no = nextId(db, 'DC', d); patch.ChallanNo = no;
    lines.forEach(function (l) { db.update('ChallanLines', l, { ChallanNo: no }); });
  }
  var fresh = lines.filter(function (l) { return blank(l.LineID) || isAppNo_(l.LineID); });
  if (fresh.length) { var ids = nextIds(db, 'CL', fresh.length); fresh.forEach(function (l, i) { db.update('ChallanLines', l, { LineID: ids[i] }); }); }
  db.update('Challans', c, patch);
  if (S(db, 'AppAutoPost', 'Yes') !== 'Yes') {
    db.update('Challans', c, { AppMsg: 'Numbered ' + no + ' — waiting for the office to post' });
    log_(db, 'App challan numbered', no, who);
    return { no: no, posted: false };
  }
  try { var r = postChallan(db, no); }
  catch (e) {
    db.update('Challans', c, { AppMsg: String(e.message).replace(/^Not posted:\n/, 'Not posted: ').replace(/\n/g, '; ').slice(0, 480) });
    log_(db, 'App challan not posted', no, who + ' · ' + e.message.replace(/\n/g, '; '));
    return { no: no, posted: false, error: e.message };
  }
  var hasPhoto = db.headers('ChallanLines').indexOf('Photo') >= 0, ph = 0;
  if (hasPhoto) db.rows('Damage').forEach(function (x) {
    if (x.ChallanNo !== no || !blank(x.Photo)) return;
    var l = lines.filter(function (y) { return y.ItemCode === x.ItemCode && !blank(y.Photo); })[0];
    if (l) { db.update('Damage', x, { Photo: l.Photo }); ph++; }
  });
  var msg = '';
  if (!o.noPdf && S(db, 'AppMakePdf', 'Yes') === 'Yes' && typeof makePdf === 'function') {
    try { makePdf(db, 'Challan', no); } catch (e) { msg = 'Posted; PDF not made: ' + e.message; }
  }
  if (msg) db.update('Challans', c, { AppMsg: msg.slice(0, 480) });
  log_(db, 'App challan posted', no, who + ' · ' + r.moves + ' moves' + (r.damage ? ' · ' + r.damage + ' damage/loss' + (ph ? ', ' + ph + ' photos' : '') : ''));
  return { no: no, posted: true, moves: r.moves, damage: r.damage, photos: ph };
}

// ======================================================================= sweep (timed trigger)
// o = { db, noPdf, deadline }. Returns { done, posted, failed, left, list }.
function appSweepRun_(o) {
  o = o || {};
  var db = o.db || new SheetDb(), out = { done: 0, posted: 0, failed: 0, left: 0, list: [] };
  var subs = db.rows('Challans').filter(function (c) { return c.Status === 'Submitted'; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return (dayNum(a.ChallanDate) || 0) - (dayNum(b.ChallanDate) || 0) || a._r - b._r; });
  subs.forEach(function (c) {
    if (timeUp_(o.deadline)) { out.left++; return; }
    var before = c.ChallanNo, r;
    try { r = appProcessChallan_(db, c, o); }
    catch (e) {
      db.update('Challans', c, { Status: 'Draft', AppMsg: ('Not posted: ' + e.message).slice(0, 480) });
      log_(db, 'App challan refused', before, (c.SubmittedBy || 'app') + ' · ' + e.message);
      r = { no: before, posted: false, error: e.message };
    }
    out.done++; if (r.posted) out.posted++; else if (r.error) out.failed++;
    out.list.push(r);
  });
  return out;
}
// Trigger handler (Tools › AppSheet › Start auto-posting). Cheap when idle: reads only the Status column.
function appSweep() {
  var db = new SheetDb(), s = db.sheet('Challans'), j = db.headers('Challans').indexOf('Status') + 1, n = s.getLastRow();
  if (j < 1 || n < 2) return;
  if (!s.getRange(2, j, n - 1, 1).getValues().some(function (r) { return r[0] === 'Submitted'; })) return;
  var lock = LockService.getDocumentLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(20000)) return; // the office is posting something; the next run picks it up
  try { appSweepRun_({ db: db, deadline: deadline_() }); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
}
function appTriggers_() { return ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(function (t) { return t.getHandlerFunction() === 'appSweep'; }); }
function appTriggerOn_(db) {
  appTriggers_().forEach(function (t) { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  var m = num(S(db, 'AppSweepMinutes', 5)); if ([1, 5, 10, 15, 30].indexOf(m) < 0) m = 5;
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('appSweep').timeBased().everyMinutes(m).create();
  return m;
}

// ======================================================================= QR labels (items / locations → PDF/Labels)
function qrLabelList_(db, kind) {
  if (kind === 'Locations') return db.rows('Locations').filter(function (l) { return !blank(l.LocID) && l.Status !== 'Inactive' && ['Yard', 'Site', 'Repair'].indexOf(l.Type) >= 0; })
    .map(function (l) { return { code: String(l.LocID), name: String(l.Name || ''), sub: String(l.Type || '') }; });
  return db.rows('Items').filter(function (i) { return !blank(i.ItemCode) && i.StockTracked === 'Yes' && i.Status !== 'Inactive'; })
    .map(function (i) { return { code: String(i.ItemCode), name: String(i.Name || ''), sub: [i.Size, i.Unit].filter(function (x) { return !blank(x); }).join(' · ') }; });
}
function qrUrl_(db, code) { return String(S(db, 'QrUrl', QR_URL_)).replace('{data}', encodeURIComponent(code)); }
function makeQrLabels(db, kind) {
  var list = qrLabelList_(db, kind);
  if (!list.length) throw new Error(kind === 'Locations' ? 'No active Yard / Site / Repair locations' : 'No active stock-tracked items (Items › StockTracked = Yes)');
  var res = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(list.map(function (x) { return { url: qrUrl_(db, x.code), muteHttpExceptions: true }; })), bad = [];
  var name = 'CAE QR labels · ' + kind + ' · ' + fmtDay(dayNum(today()));
  var folder = subFolder_(subFolder_(opsFolder_(db), 'PDF'), 'Labels');
  trashSame_(folder, name); trashSame_(folder, name + '.pdf');
  var doc = DocumentApp.create(name), body = doc.getBody(), cols = 3, rows = [];
  body.setPageWidth(595.28).setPageHeight(841.89).setMarginTop(28).setMarginBottom(28).setMarginLeft(28).setMarginRight(28);
  var hd = body.getChild(0).asParagraph(); hd.setText('Core Access Engineering Ltd. · QR labels · ' + kind + ' · ' + list.length + ' labels · cut along the lines'); hd.editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(8).setForegroundColor('#5F676E');
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i += cols) { var r = list.slice(i, i + cols).map(function () { return ''; }); while (r.length < cols) r.push(''); rows.push(r); }
  var t = body.appendTable(rows); t.setBorderColor('#D5D8DA');
  list.forEach(function (x, k) {
    var cell = t.getCell(Math.floor(k / cols), k % cols), p = cell.getChild(0).asParagraph();
    cell.setPaddingTop(8).setPaddingBottom(8);
    p.setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
    var resp = res[k];
    if (resp.getResponseCode() === 200) p.appendInlineImage(resp.getBlob()).setWidth(110).setHeight(110);
    else { p.setText('[QR not loaded]'); bad.push(x.code); }
    [[x.code, 13, true, '#000000'], [x.name, 8, false, '#2B3036'], [x.sub, 7, false, '#5F676E']].forEach(function (z) {
      if (!z[0]) return;
      var q = cell.appendParagraph(z[0]); q.setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER).setSpacingBefore(0).setSpacingAfter(0);
      q.editAsText().setFontFamily('Arial').setFontSize(z[1]).setBold(z[2]).setForegroundColor(z[3]);
    });
  });
  doc.saveAndClose();
  var f = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId()); f.moveTo(folder);
  var pdf = folder.createFile(f.getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(name + '.pdf'));
  if (S(db, 'KeepPdfDocs', 'Yes') !== 'Yes') f.setTrashed(true);
  log_(db, 'QR labels', kind, list.length + ' labels' + (bad.length ? ' · ' + bad.length + ' QR images not loaded' : ''));
  return { url: pdf.getUrl(), n: list.length, bad: bad };
}

// ======================================================================= set-up check
function appCheck_(db) {
  var out = [], ok = function (c, t, fix) { out.push((c ? 'ok   ' : 'FIX  ') + t + (c ? '' : ' — ' + fix)); };
  ok(db.headers('Challans').indexOf('AppMsg') >= 0 && db.headers('ChallanLines').indexOf('Photo') >= 0, 'O6 columns', 'run AppSheet › Upgrade sheet for O6');
  var ls = db.ss.getSheetByName('Lists'), h = ls.getRange(1, 1, 1, ls.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String), j = h.indexOf('PostStatus');
  ok(j >= 0 && ls.getRange(2, j + 1, 19, 1).getValues().some(function (r) { return r[0] === 'Submitted'; }), 'Lists PostStatus has Submitted', 'run AppSheet › Upgrade sheet for O6');
  ok(!!db.ss.getSheetByName('AppStock'), 'AppStock tab', 'run AppSheet › Upgrade sheet for O6');
  ok(appTriggers_().length === 1, 'Auto-posting trigger (' + appTriggers_().length + ')', 'AppSheet › Start auto-posting');
  ok(db.ss.getSpreadsheetLocale() === 'en_GB', 'Sheet locale ' + db.ss.getSpreadsheetLocale(), 'File › Settings › Locale = United Kingdom (matches the AppSheet table locale)');
  var noLoc = db.rows('Sites').filter(function (s) { return !blank(s.SiteID) && blank(s.LocID); }).length;
  ok(!noLoc, 'Every site has a location', noLoc + ' site(s) without LocID: Tools › Number new rows');
  var wait = db.rows('Challans').filter(function (c) { return c.Status === 'Submitted'; }).length, msg = db.rows('Challans').filter(function (c) { return c.Status === 'Draft' && !blank(c.AppMsg); }).length;
  out.push('info ' + wait + ' app challan(s) waiting · ' + msg + ' draft(s) with an app message');
  return out;
}

function menuUpgradeO6() { ui_('Upgrade sheet for O6', function (db) { var d = upgradeO6(db); return d.length ? 'Done:\n' + d.join('\n') + '\n\nNext: AppSheet › Start auto-posting.' : 'Already up to date.'; }); }
function menuAppSweep() {
  ui_('Post app challans', function (db) {
    var r = appSweepRun_({ db: db, deadline: deadline_() });
    if (!r.done) return 'No app challans waiting (Status = Submitted).';
    return r.done + ' app challan(s): ' + r.posted + ' posted, ' + r.failed + ' sent back to Draft (reason in AppMsg)' + (r.left ? ', ' + r.left + ' left for the next run' : '') + '.\n\n' +
      r.list.map(function (x) { return x.no + (x.posted ? ' posted' : x.error ? ' — ' + x.error.replace(/\n/g, '; ') : ' numbered'); }).join('\n');
  });
}
function menuAppTriggerOn() { ui_('Auto-posting', function (db) { return 'On: app challans are picked up every ' + appTriggerOn_(db) + ' minutes (Settings AppSweepMinutes).'; }); }
function menuAppTriggerOff() { ui_('Auto-posting', function () { var n = appTriggers_().length; appTriggers_().forEach(function (t) { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); }); return n ? 'Off. Use "Post app challans now" when needed.' : 'It was not on.'; }); }
function menuAppCheck() { var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); ui.alert('AppSheet set-up', appCheck_(new SheetDb()).join('\n'), ui.ButtonSet.OK); }
function qrMenu_(kind) {
  ui_('QR labels', function (db) { var r = makeQrLabels(db, kind); return r.n + ' labels: ' + r.url + (r.bad.length ? '\n\nQR images not loaded for: ' + r.bad.join(', ') + ' — run again, or change Settings QrUrl.' : ''); });
}
function menuQrItems() { qrMenu_('Items'); }
function menuQrLocations() { qrMenu_('Locations'); }

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
function menuSelfTestAll() {
  var parts = [['core', selfTest()], ['PDF', typeof selfTestPdf === 'function' ? selfTestPdf() : { pass: 0, fail: 1, log: ['FAIL CAE_Ops_PDF.gs not installed'] }],
    ['panels', typeof selfTestUi === 'function' ? selfTestUi() : { pass: 0, fail: 1, log: ['FAIL CAE_Ops_UI.gs not installed'] }],
    ['O5', typeof selfTestO5 === 'function' ? selfTestO5() : { pass: 0, fail: 1, log: ['FAIL CAE_Ops_Reports.gs not installed'] }], ['O6', selfTestO6()]];
  var p = sum(parts, function (x) { return x[1].pass; }), f = sum(parts, function (x) { return x[1].fail; });
  var fails = [].concat.apply([], parts.map(function (x) { return x[1].log; })).filter(function (l) { return /^FAIL/.test(l); });
  var setup = typeof setupCheck_ === 'function' ? setupCheck_(SpreadsheetApp.getActive()).filter(function (l) { return /^FIX/.test(l); }) : [];
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Self-test', p + ' passed, ' + f + ' failed  (' + parts.map(function (x) { return x[0] + ' ' + x[1].pass; }).join(' · ') + ')\n\n' + fails.concat(setup).join('\n'), SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK);
}
function selfTestO6() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step;
  var db = testSeed_('Full period'), t0 = dayNum(today()), D = function (k) { return fromDay(t0 - k); }, yr = String(D(0).getFullYear());
  var dc = function (n) { return 'CAE/DC/' + yr + '/' + pad(n, 3); }, JOB = 'CAE/J/2026/001', run = function (o) { return appSweepRun_(Object.assign({ db: db, noPdf: true }, o || {})); };
  var ch = function (no) { return find(db.rows('Challans'), 'ChallanNo', no); };
  var add = function (no, kind, date, lines, extra) {
    db.insert('Challans', Object.assign({ ChallanNo: no, Kind: kind, ChallanDate: date, JobNo: JOB, Status: 'Submitted', SubmittedBy: 'store@test' }, extra || {}));
    db.insert('ChallanLines', lines.map(function (l, i) { return Object.assign({ LineID: 'APP-' + no.slice(4) + i, ChallanNo: no, QtyDamaged: 0, QtyLost: 0 }, l); }));
  };
  step('U upgrade', function () {
    var d = upgradeO6(db);
    eq('U1 columns + settings added', d.join('; '), 'Challans + SubmittedBy; Challans + AppMsg; ChallanLines + Photo; Settings AppAutoPost; Settings AppMakePdf; Settings AppSweepMinutes; Settings AppMaxBackDays; Settings QrUrl');
    eq('U2 second run changes nothing', upgradeO6(db).length, 0);
    eq('U3 AppStock formula reads StockMoves by column name', appStockFormula_(db).indexOf('StockMoves!$C$2:$C&"|"&StockMoves!$F$2:$F') > 0 && appStockFormula_(db).indexOf('-StockMoves!$D$2:$D') > 0, true);
  });
  step('V app delivery', function () {
    db.insert('Jobs', { JobNo: JOB, CustID: 'CUST-0001', SiteID: 'SITE-0001', JobType: 'Rental', HireBasis: 'Item', Status: 'Awaiting order' });
    postOpeningStock(db, [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 100 }, { ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 40 }], D(20));
    add('APP-aaaa0001', 'Delivery', D(5), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 50 }, { ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 20 }]);
    db.insert('Challans', { ChallanNo: 'APP-draft001', Kind: 'Delivery', ChallanDate: D(5), JobNo: JOB, Status: 'Draft' });
    var r = run();
    eq('V1 one challan done, posted', r.done + ' ' + r.posted, '1 1');
    var c = ch(dc(1));
    eq('V2 real number + yard → site', c && c.FromLoc + ' → ' + c.ToLoc + ' ' + c.Status, 'LOC-YARD → LOC-S0001 Posted');
    eq('V3 lines renumbered with the challan', db.rows('ChallanLines').filter(function (l) { return l.ChallanNo === dc(1) && /^CL-/.test(l.LineID); }).length, 2);
    eq('V4 stock moved', stockAt(db, 'RL-STD-20', 'LOC-S0001') + ' ' + stockAt(db, 'RL-STD-20', 'LOC-YARD'), '50 50');
    eq('V5 job on hire', must(db, 'Jobs', 'JobNo', JOB).Status, 'On hire');
    eq('V6 app drafts are left alone', ch('APP-draft001').Status, 'Draft');
    eq('V7 nothing waiting → nothing done', run().done, 0);
  });
  step('W refused + fixed', function () {
    add('APP-bbbb0002', 'Delivery', D(4), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 80 }]);
    var r = run(), c = ch(dc(2));
    eq('W1 too much: numbered, back to Draft with the reason', r.failed + ' ' + (c && c.Status) + ' ' + /only 50 at LOC-YARD/.test(c && c.AppMsg), '1 Draft true');
    eq('W2 no stock moved', stockAt(db, 'RL-STD-20', 'LOC-YARD'), 50);
    db.update('ChallanLines', find(db.rows('ChallanLines'), 'ChallanNo', dc(2)), { Qty: 30 });
    db.update('Challans', c, { Status: 'Submitted', AppMsg: '' });
    r = run();
    eq('W3 resubmitted: posted under the same number', r.posted + ' ' + ch(dc(2)).Status + ' ' + (ch(dc(3)) ? 'new no.' : 'same no.'), '1 Posted same no.');
    eq('W4 yard now 20', stockAt(db, 'RL-STD-20', 'LOC-YARD'), 20);
  });
  step('X return + damage photo', function () {
    add('APP-cccc0003', 'Return', D(1), [{ ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 10, QtyDamaged: 2, QtyLost: 1, Photo: 'Photos/Damage/x1.Photo.123.jpg' }, { ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 5 }]);
    var r = run(), c = ch(dc(3)), dm = db.rows('Damage').filter(function (x) { return x.ChallanNo === dc(3); });
    eq('X1 return posted site → yard', r.posted + ' ' + c.FromLoc + ' → ' + c.ToLoc, '1 LOC-S0001 → LOC-YARD');
    eq('X2 good / repair / lost', [stockAt(db, 'BD-ST-207', 'LOC-YARD'), stockAt(db, 'BD-ST-207', 'LOC-REPAIR'), stockAt(db, 'BD-ST-207', 'LOC-LOST')].join(' '), '27 2 1');
    eq('X3 damage row has the photo', dm.length + ' ' + dm[0].Photo, '1 Photos/Damage/x1.Photo.123.jpg');
    add('APP-dddd0004', 'Return', D(1), [{ ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 50 }]);
    run();
    eq('X4 return more than on hire refused', /on hire/.test(ch(dc(4)).AppMsg) && ch(dc(4)).Status, 'Draft');
  });
  step('Y checks before numbering', function () {
    var n0 = find(db.rows('Sequences'), 'SeqKey', 'DC-' + yr).LastNo;
    add('APP-eeee0005', 'Delivery', '11/12/2026', [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    add('APP-ffff0006', 'Delivery', D(-3), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    add('APP-gggg0007', 'Delivery', D(40), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    add('APP-hhhh0008', 'Transfer', D(1), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    add('APP-iiii0009', 'Delivery', D(1), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }], { JobNo: '' });
    db.insert('Challans', { ChallanNo: 'APP-jjjj0010', Kind: 'Delivery', ChallanDate: D(1), JobNo: JOB, Status: 'Submitted' });
    var r = run();
    eq('Y1 six refused', r.done + ' ' + r.failed + ' ' + r.posted, '6 6 0');
    var m = function (no) { var c = ch(no); return c.Status + ': ' + c.AppMsg; };
    eq('Y2 text date → locale hint', /Locale = English \(United Kingdom\)/.test(m('APP-eeee0005')), true);
    eq('Y3 future date', /in the future/.test(m('APP-ffff0006')), true);
    eq('Y4 too old', /more than 31 days ago/.test(m('APP-gggg0007')), true);
    eq('Y5 transfer needs From/To', /Transfer: choose From and To/.test(m('APP-hhhh0008')), true);
    eq('Y6 job needed', /Choose the job/.test(m('APP-iiii0009')), true);
    eq('Y7 no lines', /no lines/.test(m('APP-jjjj0010')), true);
    eq('Y8 refused challans keep their APP number (no number used)', find(db.rows('Sequences'), 'SeqKey', 'DC-' + yr).LastNo, n0);
  });
  step('Z transfer, manual mode, time limit, labels', function () {
    add('APP-kkkk0011', 'Transfer', D(0), [{ ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 2 }], { FromLoc: 'LOC-REPAIR', ToLoc: 'LOC-YARD' });
    eq('Z1 transfer repair → yard posted', run().posted + ' ' + stockAt(db, 'BD-ST-207', 'LOC-REPAIR'), '1 0');
    db.update('Settings', find(db.rows('Settings'), 'Key', 'AppAutoPost'), { Value: 'No' });
    add('APP-llll0012', 'Delivery', D(0), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    var r = run(), c = db.rows('Challans').filter(function (x) { return /waiting for the office/.test(x.AppMsg); })[0];
    eq('Z2 AppAutoPost No: numbered, left Draft for the office', r.posted + ' ' + (c && c.Status) + ' ' + isAppNo_(c && c.ChallanNo), '0 Draft false');
    eq('Z3 office can post it', postChallan(db, c.ChallanNo).moves, 1);
    add('APP-mmmm0013', 'Delivery', D(0), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1 }]);
    r = run({ deadline: Date.now() - 1 });
    eq('Z4 past the time limit: left for the next run', r.done + ' ' + r.left + ' ' + ch('APP-mmmm0013').Status, '0 1 Submitted');
    eq('Z5 item labels = stock-tracked items', qrLabelList_(db, 'Items').map(function (x) { return x.code; }).join(','), 'RL-STD-20,BD-ST-207,TC-TUBE-3');
    eq('Z6 location labels = yard, repair, sites', qrLabelList_(db, 'Locations').map(function (x) { return x.code; }).join(','), 'LOC-YARD,LOC-REPAIR,LOC-S0001');
    eq('Z7 QR link encodes the code', qrUrl_(db, 'A/B 1'), QR_URL_.replace('{data}', 'A%2FB%201'));
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_Order.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — inventory ordering (stage O8) · 13 Nov 2026
 * Seventh script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_Order › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order. Then Core Access › Tools › Upgrade sheet for O8 (run once).
 * Replaces by name: onOpen (adds "Reorder" + upgrade item), menuAppSweep (Fix 6: no ":;" in the dialog).
 * Wraps: selfTestAllParts_ (adds the O8 self-test). Other files are unchanged.
 *
 * Reorder rule, per stock item with Items.ReorderLevel > 0 (sets are left out — order their parts):
 *   short = ReorderLevel − in yard − on order   (Repair does not count as available)
 *   on order = PO lines not yet received, on POs that are Draft / Sent / Part received
 *   suggest = short rounded up, at least Items.ReorderQty (minimum order) when set
 * Panel "Reorder": tick lines, adjust qty / supplier / rate → one Draft PO per supplier (yard delivery).
 * Supplier default = Items.PrefSupID; rate default = the item's last PO rate. The Reorder tab is a snapshot for
 * the office and the app (refreshed by the panel, the menu and every PO made from it).
 */
var CAE_O8_VERSION = 'O8 · 13 Nov 2026';
var REORDER_HEAD_ = ['ItemCode', 'Name', 'Unit', 'InYard', 'InRepair', 'OnOrder', 'ReorderLevel', 'Suggest', 'PrefSupID', 'LastRate', 'Updated'];

// ======================================================================= upgrade (run once, safe to repeat)
function upgradeO8(db) {
  var done = [], sh = !!db.ss;
  if (db.ensureColumn('Items', 'ReorderQty', 'ReorderLevel')) done.push('Items + ReorderQty');
  if (db.ensureColumn('Items', 'PrefSupID', 'ReorderQty')) { done.push('Items + PrefSupID'); if (sh) o7Rule_(db, 'Items', 'PrefSupID', 'Suppliers!$A$2:$A'); }
  if (db.ensureTab('Reorder', REORDER_HEAD_, [])) {
    done.push('Reorder tab');
    if (sh) db.sheet('Reorder').getRange(2, REORDER_HEAD_.length, 999, 1).setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy hh:mm');
  }
  log_(db, 'Upgrade O8', '', done.join('; ') || 'up to date');
  return done;
}
function menuUpgradeO8() { ui_('Upgrade sheet for O8', function (db) { var d = upgradeO8(db); return d.length ? 'Done:\n' + d.join('\n') + '\n\nNext: Items › ReorderLevel (and ReorderQty, PrefSupID) for the items you keep in stock.' : 'Already up to date.'; }); }

// ======================================================================= reorder list
function onOrder_(db) {
  var open = {}, got = {}, m = {};
  db.rows('POs').forEach(function (p) { if (['Draft', 'Sent', 'Part received', ''].indexOf(String(p.Status)) >= 0 && !blank(p.PONo)) open[p.PONo] = 1; });
  db.rows('StockMoves').forEach(function (v) { if (v.RefType === 'PO' && open[v.RefNo]) { var k = v.RefNo + '|' + v.ItemCode; got[k] = (got[k] || 0) + num(v.Qty); } });
  var ord = {};
  db.rows('POLines').forEach(function (l) { if (open[l.PONo] && !blank(l.ItemCode)) { var k = l.PONo + '|' + l.ItemCode; ord[k] = (ord[k] || 0) + num(l.Qty); } });
  Object.keys(ord).forEach(function (k) { var c = k.split('|')[1]; m[c] = (m[c] || 0) + Math.max(0, ord[k] - (got[k] || 0)); });
  return m;
}
function lastRates_(db) {
  var d = {}; db.rows('POs').forEach(function (p) { if (p.Status !== 'Cancelled') d[p.PONo] = dayNum(p.PODate) || 0; });
  var best = {};
  db.rows('POLines').forEach(function (l) {
    if (!(l.PONo in d) || !(num(l.Rate) > 0) || blank(l.ItemCode)) return;
    var b = best[l.ItemCode]; if (!b || d[l.PONo] >= b.d) best[l.ItemCode] = { d: d[l.PONo], r: num(l.Rate) };
  });
  var out = {}; Object.keys(best).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = best[k].r; }); return out;
}
function reorderList(db) {
  var bal = stockMap(db), oo = onOrder_(db), lr = lastRates_(db);
  return db.rows('Items').filter(function (i) {
    return !blank(i.ItemCode) && i.StockTracked === 'Yes' && !isSet_(i) && i.Status !== 'Inactive' && num(i.ReorderLevel) > 0;
  }).map(function (i) {
    var c = i.ItemCode, y = bal[c + '|' + LOC.YARD] || 0, rp = bal[c + '|' + LOC.REPAIR] || 0, o = oo[c] || 0, short = num(i.ReorderLevel) - y - o;
    return { code: c, name: i.Name, unit: i.Unit || '', yard: y, repair: rp, onOrder: o, level: num(i.ReorderLevel), short: short,
      suggest: short > 1e-9 ? Math.max(Math.ceil(short - 1e-9), num(i.ReorderQty)) : 0, sup: i.PrefSupID || '', rate: lr[c] || 0 };
  }).filter(function (x) { return x.suggest > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.code < b.code ? -1 : 1; });
}
function writeReorderTab(db, list) {
  list = list || reorderList(db);
  var now = new Date(), rows = list.map(function (x) { return [x.code, x.name, x.unit, x.yard, x.repair, x.onOrder, x.level, x.suggest, x.sup, x.rate || '', now]; });
  if (!db.ss) { db.t.Reorder = []; db.insert('Reorder', rows.map(function (r) { var o = {}; REORDER_HEAD_.forEach(function (k, j) { o[k] = r[j]; }); return o; })); return rows.length; }
  var s = db.sheet('Reorder'), n = s.getLastRow();
  if (n > 1) s.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, REORDER_HEAD_.length).clearContent();
  if (rows.length) s.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, REORDER_HEAD_.length).setValues(rows);
  db.c.Reorder = null;
  return rows.length;
}
// a = { date, lines: [{ code, qty, supId, rate }] } → one Draft PO per supplier, delivered to the yard
function makeReorderPOs(db, a) {
  var lines = (a.lines || []).filter(function (l) { return !blank(l.code) && num(l.qty) > 0; });
  if (!lines.length) throw new Error('Tick at least one item with a quantity');
  var miss = lines.filter(function (l) { return blank(l.supId); }).map(function (l) { return l.code; });
  if (miss.length) throw new Error('Choose a supplier for: ' + miss.join(', ') + ' (or set Items › PrefSupID)');
  var by = {}, order = [];
  lines.forEach(function (l) {
    var it = must(db, 'Items', 'ItemCode', l.code);
    if (it.StockTracked !== 'Yes' || isSet_(it)) throw new Error(l.code + ' is not a stock item (sets: order the parts)');
    if (num(l.rate) < 0) throw new Error(l.code + ': rate cannot be negative');
    must(db, 'Suppliers', 'SupID', l.supId);
    if (!by[l.supId]) { by[l.supId] = []; order.push(l.supId); }
    by[l.supId].push({ ItemCode: l.code, Qty: num(l.qty), Rate: num(l.rate) });
  });
  var refs = order.map(function (sup) { return uiSavePO_(db, { SupID: sup, PODate: a.date, DeliverTo: LOC.YARD, Notes: 'From reorder list', lines: by[sup] }).ref; });
  if (db.hasTab('Reorder')) writeReorderTab(db);
  log_(db, 'Reorder → POs', refs.join(', '), lines.length + ' lines');
  return { msg: refs.length + ' draft PO(s): ' + refs.join(', ') + '. Check rates, then make the PO PDF (PDF documents) and mark sent.', refs: refs };
}

// ======================================================================= panel
function o8Open() { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showSidebar(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(O8_HTML_).setTitle('Reorder')); }
function o8Init() { return uiClean_(o8InitDb_(new SheetDb())); }
function o8InitDb_(db) {
  return { today: today(), rows: reorderList(db), tab: db.hasTab('Reorder'),
    suppliers: db.rows('Suppliers').filter(function (s) { return !blank(s.SupID) && s.Status !== 'Inactive'; }).map(function (s) { return { id: s.SupID, name: s.Name }; }) };
}
var O8_ACTIONS_ = {
  makePOs: function (db, a) { return makeReorderPOs(db, a); },
  writeTab: function (db) { if (!db.hasTab('Reorder')) throw new Error('Run Tools › Upgrade sheet for O8 first'); return writeReorderTab(db) + ' item(s) written to the Reorder tab.'; },
};
function o8Do(action, args) {
  return uiClean_(withLock(function () {
    var f = O8_ACTIONS_[action]; if (!f) throw new Error('Unknown action ' + action);
    var r = f(new SheetDb(), args || {}); SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return typeof r === 'string' ? { msg: r } : r;
  }));
}
function menuReorderTab() { ui_('Reorder list', function (db) { if (!db.hasTab('Reorder')) return 'Run Tools › Upgrade sheet for O8 first.'; var n = writeReorderTab(db); return n ? n + ' item(s) below their reorder level — see the Reorder tab, or open Core Access › Reorder to make POs.' : 'Nothing to order: every item with a ReorderLevel is covered by yard stock + open POs.'; }); }

// ======================================================================= Fix 6: sweep dialog without ":;"
function sweepLine_(x) { return x.no + (x.posted ? ' posted' : x.error ? ' — ' + String(x.error).replace(/:\s*\n/g, ': ').replace(/\n/g, '; ') : ' numbered'); }
function menuAppSweep() {
  ui_('Post app challans + conversions', function (db) {
    var r = appSweepRun_({ db: db, deadline: deadline_() });
    if (!r.done) return 'Nothing waiting from the app (Status = Submitted).';
    return r.done + ' from the app: ' + r.posted + ' posted, ' + r.failed + ' sent back to Draft (reason in AppMsg)' + (r.left ? ', ' + r.left + ' left for the next run' : '') + '.\n\n' + r.list.map(sweepLine_).join('\n');
  });
}

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var O8_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;
  selfTestAllParts_ = function () { return O8_parts0_().concat([['O8', selfTestO8()]]); };
}
function selfTestO8() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws;
  var db = testSeed_('Full period');
  var item = function (code, p) { db.update('Items', must(db, 'Items', 'ItemCode', code), p); };
  var list = function () { return reorderList(db).map(function (x) { return x.code + ' ' + x.suggest; }).join(', ') || 'none'; };
  step('N upgrade', function () {
    upgradeO6(db); upgradeO7(db);
    eq('N1 columns + tab', upgradeO8(db).join(', '), 'Items + ReorderQty, Items + PrefSupID, Reorder tab');
    eq('N2 second run changes nothing', upgradeO8(db).length, 0);
    eq('N3 columns in order', db.headers('Items').slice(db.headers('Items').indexOf('ReorderLevel'), db.headers('Items').indexOf('ReorderLevel') + 3).join(','), 'ReorderLevel,ReorderQty,PrefSupID');
  });
  step('N reorder list', function () {
    eq('N4 no levels = nothing to order', list(), 'none');
    item('RL-STD-20', { ReorderLevel: 50, ReorderQty: 100, PrefSupID: 'SUP-0001' }); item('BD-ST-207', { ReorderLevel: 30 }); item('TC-TUBE-3', { ReorderLevel: 10 });
    item('SV-TRANS', { ReorderLevel: 5 }); item('AM-SET-D725', { ReorderLevel: 2 });
    postOpeningStock(db, [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 20 }, { ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 5, LocID: 'LOC-REPAIR' }, { ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 40 }], '01-Nov-2026');
    eq('N5 short items only; min order qty; repair, services and sets not counted', list(), 'RL-STD-20 100, TC-TUBE-3 10');
    var r = reorderList(db)[0];
    eq('N6 row detail', [r.yard, r.repair, r.onOrder, r.level, r.short, r.sup].join(' '), '20 5 0 50 30 SUP-0001');
    item('TC-TUBE-3', { ReorderLevel: 10.5 });
    eq('N7 part quantities rounded up', reorderList(db)[1].suggest, 11);
    item('TC-TUBE-3', { ReorderLevel: 10 });
  });
  step('N on order + POs', function () {
    db.insert('POs', [{ PONo: 'CAE/PO/2026/090', SupID: 'SUP-0001', PODate: toDate('01-Nov-2026'), DeliverTo: 'LOC-YARD', Status: 'Sent', VATPct: 0 },
      { PONo: 'CAE/PO/2026/091', SupID: 'SUP-0001', PODate: toDate('02-Nov-2026'), DeliverTo: 'LOC-YARD', Status: 'Cancelled', VATPct: 0 }]);
    db.insert('POLines', [{ LineID: 'POL-09001', PONo: 'CAE/PO/2026/090', ItemCode: 'TC-TUBE-3', Qty: 6, Rate: 900 }, { LineID: 'POL-09002', PONo: 'CAE/PO/2026/091', ItemCode: 'TC-TUBE-3', Qty: 50, Rate: 950 }]);
    eq('N8 open PO counts as on order, cancelled PO ignored', list(), 'RL-STD-20 100, TC-TUBE-3 4');
    eq('N9 last rate from a live PO (cancelled skipped)', reorderList(db)[1].rate, 900);
    receivePO(db, 'CAE/PO/2026/090', [{ itemCode: 'TC-TUBE-3', qty: 2 }], '05-Nov-2026');
    eq('N10 part received: 2 in yard + 4 still on order', reorderList(db)[1].yard + ' ' + reorderList(db)[1].onOrder + ' ' + reorderList(db)[1].suggest, '2 4 4');
    throws('N11 no supplier refused, names the item', function () { makeReorderPOs(db, { date: '2026-11-13', lines: [{ code: 'TC-TUBE-3', qty: 4, supId: '' }] }); }, /supplier for: TC-TUBE-3/);
    throws('N12 nothing ticked refused', function () { makeReorderPOs(db, { lines: [{ code: 'TC-TUBE-3', qty: 0, supId: 'SUP-0001' }] }); }, /at least one/);
    throws('N13 set refused', function () { makeReorderPOs(db, { lines: [{ code: 'AM-SET-D725', qty: 1, supId: 'SUP-0001' }] }); }, /not a stock item/);
    throws('N14 unknown supplier refused', function () { makeReorderPOs(db, { lines: [{ code: 'TC-TUBE-3', qty: 1, supId: 'SUP-9' }] }); }, /not found/);
    eq('N15 refused calls made no PO', db.rows('POs').length, 2);
    db.insert('Suppliers', { SupID: 'SUP-0002', Name: 'Tube Mills', Status: 'Active' });
    var r = makeReorderPOs(db, { date: '2026-11-13', lines: [{ code: 'RL-STD-20', qty: 100, supId: 'SUP-0001', rate: 2500 }, { code: 'TC-TUBE-3', qty: 4, supId: 'SUP-0002', rate: '' }] });
    eq('N16 one draft PO per supplier', r.refs.join(' '), 'CAE/PO/2026/001 CAE/PO/2026/002');
    var p1 = must(db, 'POs', 'PONo', r.refs[0]), l1 = db.rows('POLines').filter(function (l) { return l.PONo === r.refs[0]; });
    eq('N17 PO header + line', [p1.Status, p1.DeliverTo, p1.SupID, l1.length, l1[0].ItemCode, l1[0].Qty, l1[0].Rate, l1[0].Unit].join(' '), 'Draft LOC-YARD SUP-0001 1 RL-STD-20 100 2500 pc');
    eq('N18 draft POs cover the shortfall: nothing left to order', list(), 'none');
    eq('N19 Reorder tab rewritten (empty now)', db.rows('Reorder').length, 0);
    db.update('POs', p1, { Status: 'Cancelled' });
    eq('N20 cancelling the PO brings the item back', list(), 'RL-STD-20 100');
    eq('N21 Reorder tab snapshot', writeReorderTab(db) + ' ' + db.rows('Reorder')[0].ItemCode + ' ' + db.rows('Reorder')[0].Suggest, '1 RL-STD-20 100');
  });
  step('N fix 6 + panel', function () {
    eq('N22 sweep dialog line has no ":;"', sweepLine_({ no: 'CAE/DC/2026/009', error: 'Not posted:\nRL-STD-20: only 0 at LOC-YARD\nBD-ST-207: qty must be > 0' }),
      'CAE/DC/2026/009 — Not posted: RL-STD-20: only 0 at LOC-YARD; BD-ST-207: qty must be > 0');
    var js = O8_HTML_.slice(O8_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, O8_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('N23 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    var used = {}, re = /act\('(\w+)'/g, m; while ((m = re.exec(O8_HTML_))) used[m[1]] = 1;
    eq('N24 every panel action exists on the server', Object.keys(used).filter(function (k) { return !O8_ACTIONS_[k]; }).join(',') || 'none', 'none');
    var init = uiClean_(o8InitDb_(db));
    eq('N25 panel data', init.rows.length + ' ' + init.suppliers.length + ' ' + init.tab + ' ' + (JSON.stringify(init).indexOf('"_r"') < 0), '1 2 true true');
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_QuoteReq.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — site quote requests (add-on QR1) · 13 Nov 2026
 * Eighth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_QuoteReq › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq. Then Core Access › Tools › Upgrade sheet for quote requests (run once).
 * Replaces by name: onOpen (adds "Quote requests from site" + the upgrade item). Wraps: selfTestAllParts_ (adds the QR self-test).
 *
 * Field staff add requests in the CAE Field app (tab QuoteReqs, Status New). Office: Core Access › Quote requests from site:
 *   Make draft quote = customer (added if the request names a new one), site (added if new), quote type = work type,
 *   one line with the request details (rate 0) → Draft quote; request → Quoted + QuoteNo. Then price the lines in Quotes › Edit.
 *   Drop = request → Dropped with the office note.
 */
var CAE_QR_VERSION = 'QR1 · 13 Nov 2026';
var QR_HEAD_ = ['ReqID', 'ReqDate', 'StaffID', 'CustID', 'NewCustomer', 'SiteID', 'NewSite', 'Contact', 'Phone', 'WorkType', 'Details', 'LengthM', 'WidthM', 'HeightM', 'Photo', 'NeededBy', 'Status', 'QuoteNo', 'OfficeNote'];
var QR_STATUS_ = ['New', 'Quoted', 'Dropped'];

// ======================================================================= upgrade (run once, safe to repeat)
function upgradeQR(db) {
  var done = [];
  if (db.ensureTab('QuoteReqs', QR_HEAD_, [])) {
    done.push('QuoteReqs tab');
    if (db.ss) {
      var s = db.sheet('QuoteReqs'), col = function (c) { return s.getRange(2, QR_HEAD_.indexOf(c) + 1, 998, 1); };
      var list = function (v) { return SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().setAllowInvalid(false).requireValueInList(v, true).build(); };
      col('WorkType').setDataValidation(list(UI_LISTS_DEFAULT_.QuoteType));
      col('Status').setDataValidation(list(QR_STATUS_));
      [['CustID', 'Customers'], ['SiteID', 'Sites'], ['StaffID', 'Staff']].forEach(function (x) {
        col(x[0]).setDataValidation(SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().setAllowInvalid(true).requireValueInRange(db.sheet(x[1]).getRange('A2:A'), true).build());
      });
      col('ReqDate').setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy'); col('NeededBy').setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy'); col('Phone').setNumberFormat('@');
    }
  }
  log_(db, 'Upgrade QR', '', done.join('; ') || 'up to date');
  return done;
}
function menuUpgradeQR() { ui_('Upgrade sheet for quote requests', function (db) { var d = upgradeQR(db); return d.length ? 'Done: ' + d.join(', ') + '.\n\nNext: add the QuoteReqs table to the CAE Field app.' : 'Already up to date.'; }); }

// ======================================================================= requests
function qrDims_(r) { var d = [['L', 'LengthM'], ['W', 'WidthM'], ['H', 'HeightM']].filter(function (k) { return num(r[k[1]]) > 0; }).map(function (k) { return k[0] + ' ' + num(r[k[1]]); }); return d.length ? d.join(' × ') + ' m' : ''; }
function qrList(db) {
  var get = function (t, k, id) { return blank(id) ? null : find(db.rows(t), k, id); };
  return db.rows('QuoteReqs').filter(function (r) { return !blank(r.ReqID); }).map(function (r) {
    var c = get('Customers', 'CustID', r.CustID), s = get('Sites', 'SiteID', r.SiteID), st = get('Staff', 'StaffID', r.StaffID);
    return { id: r.ReqID, date: blank(r.ReqDate) ? '' : fmtDay(dayNum(r.ReqDate)), by: st ? st.Name : (r.StaffID || ''),
      cust: c ? c.Name : (blank(r.NewCustomer) ? '' : 'New: ' + r.NewCustomer), site: s ? (s.Project || s.Address) : (blank(r.NewSite) ? '' : 'New: ' + r.NewSite),
      contact: [r.Contact, r.Phone].filter(function (x) { return !blank(x); }).join(' · '), type: r.WorkType || '', details: r.Details || '', dims: qrDims_(r), photo: !blank(r.Photo),
      needed: blank(r.NeededBy) ? '' : fmtDay(dayNum(r.NeededBy)), status: r.Status || 'New', quote: r.QuoteNo || '', note: r.OfficeNote || '' };
  }).reverse();
}
function qrNew_(db, id) {
  var r = must(db, 'QuoteReqs', 'ReqID', id), st = r.Status || 'New';
  if (st !== 'New') throw new Error(id + ' is ' + st + (r.QuoteNo ? ' (' + r.QuoteNo + ')' : ''));
  return r;
}
// o = { date } → Draft quote; new customer / site added first and written back to the request
function quoteFromRequest(db, id, o) {
  o = o || {};
  var r = qrNew_(db, id), cust = r.CustID, site = r.SiteID, added = [];
  if (UI_LISTS_DEFAULT_.QuoteType.indexOf(String(r.WorkType)) < 0) throw new Error(id + ': work type must be ' + UI_LISTS_DEFAULT_.QuoteType.join(' / '));
  if (blank(r.Details)) throw new Error(id + ': no details — what is needed?');
  if (blank(cust) && blank(r.NewCustomer)) throw new Error(id + ': no customer — pick one or type a new customer name');
  if (blank(cust) && !blank(site)) throw new Error(id + ': site ' + site + ' is picked but the customer is new — pick the customer too');
  if (!blank(cust)) must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', cust);
  if (!blank(site) && must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', site).CustID !== cust) throw new Error(id + ': site ' + site + ' belongs to another customer');
  if (blank(cust)) {
    try { cust = uiSaveCustomer_(db, { name: r.NewCustomer, contact: r.Contact, phone: r.Phone }).ref; }
    catch (e) { throw new Error(id + ': ' + e.message + ' — pick it as the customer on the request'); }
    added.push('customer ' + cust);
  }
  if (blank(site) && !blank(r.NewSite)) { site = uiSaveSite_(db, { custId: cust, project: r.NewSite, address: r.NewSite, siteRep: r.Contact, siteRepPhone: r.Phone }); added.push('site ' + site); }
  var dims = qrDims_(r), st = blank(r.StaffID) ? null : find(db.rows('Staff'), 'StaffID', r.StaffID);
  var q = uiSaveQuote_(db, {
    header: { QuoteType: r.WorkType, QuoteDate: o.date || '', CustID: cust, SiteID: site || '', Attention: r.Contact || '',
      Notes: 'From site request ' + id + (st ? ' by ' + st.Name : '') + (blank(r.NeededBy) ? '' : ' · needed by ' + fmtDay(dayNum(r.NeededBy))) },
    lines: [{ Description: 'Site request ' + id + ': ' + String(r.Details).trim() + (dims ? ' (' + dims + ')' : ''), Qty: 1, Rate: 0 }],
  });
  uiPutRow_(db, 'QuoteReqs', r, { CustID: cust, SiteID: site || '', Status: 'Quoted', QuoteNo: q.ref });
  log_(db, 'Request → quote', id, q.ref + (added.length ? ' · added ' + added.join(', ') : ''));
  return { msg: q.ref + ' created as Draft from ' + id + (added.length ? ' (added ' + added.join(', ') + ')' : '') + '. Open it in Quotes › Edit to add the priced lines.', ref: q.ref };
}
function dropRequest(db, id, note) {
  var r = qrNew_(db, id);
  uiPutRow_(db, 'QuoteReqs', r, { Status: 'Dropped', OfficeNote: note || '' });
  log_(db, 'Request dropped', id, note || '');
  return id + ' dropped.';
}

// ======================================================================= panel
function qrOpen() { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showSidebar(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(QR_HTML_).setTitle('Quote requests')); }
function qrOpenQuote(no) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showSidebar(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(uiHtml_('quotes', no)).setTitle('Core Access')); }
function qrInit() { return uiClean_(qrInitDb_(new SheetDb())); }
function qrInitDb_(db) {
  if (!db.hasTab('QuoteReqs')) return { rows: [], tab: false };
  var all = qrList(db);
  return { tab: true, rows: all.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'New'; }).concat(all.filter(function (r) { return r.status !== 'New'; }).slice(0, 15)) };
}
var QR_ACTIONS_ = {
  makeQuote: function (db, a) { return quoteFromRequest(db, a.id, {}); },
  drop: function (db, a) { return dropRequest(db, a.id, a.note); },
};
function qrDo(action, args) {
  return uiClean_(withLock(function () {
    var f = QR_ACTIONS_[action]; if (!f) throw new Error('Unknown action ' + action);
    var r = f(new SheetDb(), args || {}); SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return typeof r === 'string' ? { msg: r } : r;
  }));
}
var QR_HTML_ = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><base target="_top"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var QR_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;
  selfTestAllParts_ = function () { return QR_parts0_().concat([['QR', selfTestQR()]]); };
}
function selfTestQR() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws;
  var db = testSeed_('Full period'), add = function (o) { db.insert('QuoteReqs', Object.assign({ ReqDate: toDate('13-Nov-2026'), Status: 'New' }, o)); };
  var req = function (id) { return must(db, 'QuoteReqs', 'ReqID', id); };
  step('R upgrade', function () {
    eq('R1 tab added', upgradeQR(db).join(), 'QuoteReqs tab');
    eq('R2 second run changes nothing', upgradeQR(db).length, 0);
    eq('R3 columns', db.headers('QuoteReqs').join(','), QR_HEAD_.join(','));
  });
  step('R make quote', function () {
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-a1', CustID: 'CUST-0001', SiteID: 'SITE-0001', Contact: 'Mr Karim', Phone: '01711000000', WorkType: 'Rental', Details: 'Ringlock for tank 12 m dia', LengthM: 12, WidthM: 12, HeightM: 9.5, NeededBy: toDate('20-Nov-2026') });
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-b2', NewCustomer: 'Delta Builders Ltd.', NewSite: 'Tongi plant', Contact: 'Engr Rahim', Phone: '01811000000', WorkType: 'Project', Details: 'Facade scaffold 40 m x 20 m' });
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-c3', CustID: 'CUST-0001', WorkType: 'Painting', Details: 'Paint the tank' });
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-d4', WorkType: 'Sale', Details: 'Tubes' });
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-e5', NewCustomer: 'test client', WorkType: 'Sale', Details: 'Tubes' });
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-f6', NewCustomer: 'Zeta Ltd.', SiteID: 'SITE-0001', WorkType: 'Sale', Details: 'Tubes' });
    var r = quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-a1', { date: '2026-11-13' }), q = must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', r.ref), l = db.rows('QuoteLines').filter(function (x) { return x.QuoteNo === r.ref; });
    eq('R4 draft quote from the request', [r.ref, q.Status, q.QuoteType, q.CustID, q.SiteID, q.Attention, q.MinMonths].join(' '), 'CAE/Q/2026/001 Draft Rental CUST-0001 SITE-0001 Mr Karim 1');
    eq('R5 one line with the details, rate 0', l.length + ' | ' + l[0].Description + ' | ' + l[0].Qty + ' ' + l[0].Rate, '1 | Site request QR-a1: Ringlock for tank 12 m dia (L 12 × W 12 × H 9.5 m) | 1 0');
    eq('R6 quote note', q.Notes, 'From site request QR-a1 · needed by 20-Nov-2026');
    eq('R7 request marked Quoted', req('QR-a1').Status + ' ' + req('QR-a1').QuoteNo, 'Quoted CAE/Q/2026/001');
    throws('R8 second quote refused', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-a1'); }, /is Quoted \(CAE\/Q\/2026\/001\)/);
    r = quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-b2', { date: '2026-11-13' });
    var b2 = req('QR-b2'), c = must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', b2.CustID), s = must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', b2.SiteID);
    eq('R9 new customer + site added and written back', [b2.CustID, c.Name, c.Phone, s.Project, s.CustID, s.LocID].join(' | '), 'CUST-0002 | Delta Builders Ltd. | 01811000000 | Tongi plant | CUST-0002 | LOC-S0002');
    eq('R10 project quote for the new customer', must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', r.ref).QuoteType + ' ' + must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', r.ref).CustID + ' ' + r.ref, 'Project CUST-0002 CAE/Q/2026/002');
    throws('R11 unknown work type refused', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-c3'); }, /work type must be/);
    throws('R12 no customer refused', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-d4'); }, /no customer/);
    throws('R13 existing customer name refused with a hint', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-e5'); }, /already exists — pick it/);
    throws('R14 picked site with a new customer refused', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-f6'); }, /customer is new/);
    eq('R15 refused requests made nothing', db.rows('Quotes').length + ' ' + db.rows('Customers').length + ' ' + db.rows('Sites').length, '2 2 2');
  });
  step('R drop + panel', function () {
    eq('R16 drop', dropRequest(db, 'QR-c3', 'Painting is not our work'), 'QR-c3 dropped.');
    eq('R17 dropped with the note', req('QR-c3').Status + ' | ' + req('QR-c3').OfficeNote, 'Dropped | Painting is not our work');
    throws('R18 dropped cannot be quoted', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-c3'); }, /is Dropped/);
    var L = qrList(db), a = find(L, 'id', 'QR-a1');
    eq('R19 list newest first, names + dims', L[0].id + ' ' + L.length + ' | ' + a.cust + ' | ' + a.site + ' | ' + a.dims + ' | ' + a.needed, 'QR-f6 6 | Test Client | Tower A | L 12 × W 12 × H 9.5 m | 20-Nov-2026');
    var js = QR_HTML_.slice(QR_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, QR_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('R20 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    var used = {}, re = /act\('(\w+)'/g, m; while ((m = re.exec(QR_HTML_))) used[m[1]] = 1;
    eq('R21 every panel action exists on the server', Object.keys(used).sort().join(',') + ' ' + (Object.keys(used).filter(function (k) { return !QR_ACTIONS_[k]; }).join(',') || 'none'), 'drop,makeQuote none');
    var init = uiClean_(qrInitDb_(db));
    eq('R22 panel data: new first, safe for the panel', init.rows.map(function (x) { return x.status[0]; }).join('') + ' ' + (JSON.stringify(init).indexOf('"_r"') < 0), 'NNNDQQ true');
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_Site.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — site inspections + expense claims with photos (add-on IE1) · 13 Nov 2026
 * Ninth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_Site › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq, Site. Then Core Access › Tools › Upgrade sheet for inspections + claims (run once).
 * Replaces by name: onOpen (adds "Site inspections + expense claims" + the upgrade item). Wraps: selfTestAllParts_ (adds the IE self-test).
 *
 * Field staff, in the CAE Field app:
 *   Inspections   — scaffold inspection on a job (Weekly / Handover / After bad weather / After change), result Safe / Fixed on site / Unsafe, up to 2 photos.
 *   ExpenseClaims — money spent for a job or the office, with a receipt photo (required). Status Submitted.
 * Office: Core Access › Site inspections + expense claims:
 *   Unsafe inspections (open until the office closes them with a note) · jobs on hire with no inspection in the last InspectionDays (Settings, default 7) ·
 *   claims: Approve = picks the payment method (no cash) → new Expenses row (EXP-, receipt photo copied, ApprovedBy = office staff), claim Approved + ExpID; Reject + note.
 *   Only approved claims reach Expenses, so the reports never count a claim that is still waiting.
 */
var CAE_IE_VERSION = 'IE1 · 13 Nov 2026';
var IN_HEAD_ = ['InspID', 'InspDate', 'StaffID', 'JobNo', 'Kind', 'Area', 'Result', 'Findings', 'Photo', 'Photo2', 'ClosedDate', 'OfficeNote'];
var IN_KIND_ = ['Weekly', 'Handover', 'After bad weather', 'After change', 'Dismantle'];
var IN_RESULT_ = ['Safe', 'Fixed on site', 'Unsafe'];
var CL_HEAD_ = ['ClaimID', 'ClaimDate', 'StaffID', 'JobNo', 'Category', 'Payee', 'Amount', 'Details', 'ReceiptPhoto', 'Status', 'ExpID', 'OfficeNote'];
var CL_STATUS_ = ['Submitted', 'Approved', 'Rejected'];
var IE_CATS_ = ['Transport', 'Labour', 'Fuel', 'Site expenses', 'Repairs', 'Office', 'Utilities', 'Other'];

// ======================================================================= upgrade (run once, safe to repeat)
function upgradeIE(db) {
  var done = [];
  var list = function (v) { return SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().setAllowInvalid(false).requireValueInList(v, true).build(); };
  var ref = function (t) { return SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().setAllowInvalid(true).requireValueInRange(db.sheet(t).getRange('A2:A'), true).build(); };
  var cols = function (t, head) { var s = db.sheet(t); return function (c) { return s.getRange(2, head.indexOf(c) + 1, 998, 1); }; };
  if (db.ensureTab('Inspections', IN_HEAD_, [])) {
    done.push('Inspections tab');
    if (db.ss) {
      var c = cols('Inspections', IN_HEAD_);
      c('Kind').setDataValidation(list(IN_KIND_)); c('Result').setDataValidation(list(IN_RESULT_));
      c('JobNo').setDataValidation(ref('Jobs')); c('StaffID').setDataValidation(ref('Staff'));
      c('InspDate').setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy'); c('ClosedDate').setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy');
    }
  }
  if (db.ensureTab('ExpenseClaims', CL_HEAD_, [])) {
    done.push('ExpenseClaims tab');
    if (db.ss) {
      var e = cols('ExpenseClaims', CL_HEAD_);
      e('Category').setDataValidation(list(IE_CATS_)); e('Status').setDataValidation(list(CL_STATUS_));
      e('JobNo').setDataValidation(ref('Jobs')); e('StaffID').setDataValidation(ref('Staff'));
      e('ClaimDate').setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy'); e('Amount').setNumberFormat('#,##0.00');
    }
  }
  if (!find(db.rows('Settings'), 'Key', 'InspectionDays')) {
    db.insert('Settings', { Key: 'InspectionDays', Value: 7, Notes: 'Scaffold on hire: inspection due after this many days (site app › Inspections)' });
    done.push('Settings InspectionDays 7');
  }
  log_(db, 'Upgrade IE', '', done.join('; ') || 'up to date');
  return done;
}
function menuUpgradeIE() { ui_('Upgrade sheet for inspections + claims', function (db) { var d = upgradeIE(db); return d.length ? 'Done: ' + d.join(', ') + '.\n\nNext: add the Inspections and ExpenseClaims tables to the CAE Field app.' : 'Already up to date.'; }); }

// ======================================================================= helpers
function ieDay_(v) { return blank(v) ? '' : fmtDay(dayNum(v)); }
function ieStaff_(db, id) { var s = blank(id) ? null : find(db.rows('Staff'), 'StaffID', id); return s ? s.Name : (id || ''); }
function ieJob_(db, no) {
  var j = blank(no) ? null : find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'JobNo', no); if (!j) return no || '';
  var c = find(db.rows('Customers'), 'CustID', j.CustID), s = find(db.rows('Sites'), 'SiteID', j.SiteID);
  return j.JobNo + (c ? ' · ' + c.Name : '') + (s ? ' · ' + (s.Project || s.Address) : '');
}
function ieMe_(db, by) {
  if (!blank(by)) return must(db, 'Staff', 'StaffID', by).StaffID;
  var me = String(user_()).toLowerCase(), s = db.rows('Staff').filter(function (x) { return String(x.Email || '').toLowerCase() === me; })[0];
  return s ? s.StaffID : '';
}

// ======================================================================= inspections
function inspIsOpen_(r) { return r.Result === 'Unsafe' && blank(r.ClosedDate); }
function inspList(db) {
  return db.rows('Inspections').filter(function (r) { return !blank(r.InspID); }).map(function (r) {
    return { id: r.InspID, date: ieDay_(r.InspDate), d: dayNum(r.InspDate) || 0, by: ieStaff_(db, r.StaffID), job: ieJob_(db, r.JobNo), jobNo: r.JobNo || '', kind: r.Kind || '', area: r.Area || '',
      result: r.Result || '', findings: r.Findings || '', photos: [r.Photo, r.Photo2].filter(function (x) { return !blank(x); }).length,
      open: inspIsOpen_(r), closed: ieDay_(r.ClosedDate), note: r.OfficeNote || '' };
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.d - a.d || (a.id < b.id ? 1 : -1); });
}
// Jobs with scaffold on site (Rental / Project, On hire) and no inspection within InspectionDays (Unsafe ones count as inspected; they show in the open list).
function inspDue(db, asOf) {
  var d = dayNum(asOf || today()), every = num(S(db, 'InspectionDays', 7)) || 7, last = {};
  db.rows('Inspections').forEach(function (r) { var x = dayNum(r.InspDate); if (x != null && !blank(r.JobNo)) last[r.JobNo] = Math.max(last[r.JobNo] || -1e9, x); });
  return db.rows('Jobs').filter(function (j) { return j.Status === 'On hire' && (j.JobType === 'Rental' || j.JobType === 'Project'); }).map(function (j) {
    var l = last[j.JobNo], since = hireStartDay(db, j.JobNo), from = l != null ? l : since;
    return { jobNo: j.JobNo, job: ieJob_(db, j.JobNo), last: l != null ? fmtDay(l) : '', days: from != null ? d - from : null };
  }).filter(function (x) { return x.days == null || x.days >= every; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.days || 0) - (a.days || 0); });
}
function closeInspection(db, id, note, date) {
  var r = must(db, 'Inspections', 'InspID', id);
  if (r.Result !== 'Unsafe') throw new Error(id + ' is ' + (r.Result || 'not marked') + ' — only Unsafe inspections need closing');
  if (!blank(r.ClosedDate)) throw new Error(id + ' is already closed (' + ieDay_(r.ClosedDate) + ')');
  if (blank(note)) throw new Error('Say what was done (note) before closing ' + id);
  db.update('Inspections', r, { ClosedDate: date ? toDate(date) : today(), OfficeNote: note });
  log_(db, 'Inspection closed', id, (r.JobNo || '') + ' · ' + note);
  return id + ' closed.';
}

// ======================================================================= expense claims
function claimList(db) {
  return db.rows('ExpenseClaims').filter(function (r) { return !blank(r.ClaimID); }).map(function (r) {
    return { id: r.ClaimID, date: ieDay_(r.ClaimDate), d: dayNum(r.ClaimDate) || 0, by: ieStaff_(db, r.StaffID), job: r.JobNo ? ieJob_(db, r.JobNo) : 'Office / no job',
      cat: r.Category || '', payee: r.Payee || '', amount: num(r.Amount), details: r.Details || '', photo: !blank(r.ReceiptPhoto),
      status: r.Status || 'Submitted', exp: r.ExpID || '', note: r.OfficeNote || '' };
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.d - a.d || (a.id < b.id ? 1 : -1); });
}
function claimNew_(db, id) {
  var r = must(db, 'ExpenseClaims', 'ClaimID', id), st = r.Status || 'Submitted';
  if (st !== 'Submitted') throw new Error(id + ' is ' + st + (r.ExpID ? ' (' + r.ExpID + ')' : ''));
  return r;
}
// o = { method, refNo, date, by } → Expenses row; the claim keeps the link (ExpID)
function approveClaim(db, id, o) {
  o = o || {};
  var r = claimNew_(db, id), method = o.method || 'Bank transfer';
  if (PAY_METHODS.indexOf(method) < 0) throw new Error('Method must be: ' + PAY_METHODS.join(', ') + ' (no cash)');
  if (!(num(r.Amount) > 0)) throw new Error(id + ': amount must be more than 0');
  if (IE_CATS_.indexOf(String(r.Category)) < 0) throw new Error(id + ': category must be ' + IE_CATS_.join(' / '));
  if (blank(r.Payee)) throw new Error(id + ': payee is missing');
  if (blank(r.ReceiptPhoto)) throw new Error(id + ': no receipt photo');
  if (!blank(r.JobNo)) must(db, 'Jobs', 'JobNo', r.JobNo);
  var exp = nextId(db, 'EXP'), by = ieMe_(db, o.by), who = ieStaff_(db, r.StaffID);
  db.insert('Expenses', { ExpID: exp, ExpDate: o.date ? toDate(o.date) : (blank(r.ClaimDate) ? today() : toDate(r.ClaimDate)), Category: r.Category, JobNo: r.JobNo || '', Payee: r.Payee,
    Amount: num(r.Amount), Method: method, RefNo: o.refNo || '', ReceiptPhoto: r.ReceiptPhoto, ApprovedBy: by,
    Notes: 'Site claim ' + id + (who ? ' by ' + who : '') + (blank(r.Details) ? '' : ': ' + r.Details) });
  db.update('ExpenseClaims', r, { Status: 'Approved', ExpID: exp, OfficeNote: o.note || r.OfficeNote || '' });
  log_(db, 'Claim approved', id, exp + ' · Tk ' + num(r.Amount) + (r.JobNo ? ' · ' + r.JobNo : ''));
  return { msg: id + ' approved → ' + exp + ' (Tk ' + num(r.Amount) + ', ' + method + ').', ref: exp };
}
function rejectClaim(db, id, note) {
  var r = claimNew_(db, id);
  if (blank(note)) throw new Error('Give a reason before rejecting ' + id);
  db.update('ExpenseClaims', r, { Status: 'Rejected', OfficeNote: note });
  log_(db, 'Claim rejected', id, note);
  return id + ' rejected.';
}

// ======================================================================= panel
function ieOpen() { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showSidebar(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(IE_HTML_).setTitle('Inspections + claims')); }
function ieClean_(x) { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)); }
function ieInit() { return ieClean_(ieInitDb_(new SheetDb())); }
function ieInitDb_(db, asOf) {
  if (!db.hasTab('Inspections') || !db.hasTab('ExpenseClaims')) return { tab: false };
  var ins = inspList(db), cl = claimList(db);
  return { tab: true, methods: PAY_METHODS.slice(), every: num(S(db, 'InspectionDays', 7)) || 7,
    open: ins.filter(function (r) { return r.open; }), due: inspDue(db, asOf), recent: ins.filter(function (r) { return !r.open; }).slice(0, 10),
    claims: cl.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'Submitted'; }), done: cl.filter(function (r) { return r.status !== 'Submitted'; }).slice(0, 10) };
}
var IE_ACTIONS_ = {
  closeInsp: function (db, a) { return closeInspection(db, a.id, a.note); },
  approve: function (db, a) { return approveClaim(db, a.id, { method: a.method, refNo: a.refNo }); },
  reject: function (db, a) { return rejectClaim(db, a.id, a.note); },
};
function ieDo(action, args) {
  return ieClean_(withLock(function () {
    var f = IE_ACTIONS_[action]; if (!f) throw new Error('Unknown action ' + action);
    var r = f(new SheetDb(), args || {}); SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return typeof r === 'string' ? { msg: r } : r;
  }));
}

if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var IE_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;
  selfTestAllParts_ = function () { return IE_parts0_().concat([['IE', selfTestIE()]]); };

function selfTestIE() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws;
  var db = testSeed_('Full period'), job = '';
  var ins = function (o) { db.insert('Inspections', Object.assign({ StaffID: 'STF-001', Kind: 'Weekly' }, o)); };
  var cl = function (o) { db.insert('ExpenseClaims', Object.assign({ ClaimDate: toDate('12-Nov-2026'), StaffID: 'STF-001', Category: 'Transport', Payee: 'Truck owner', Amount: 3500, ReceiptPhoto: 'ExpenseClaims_Images/a.jpg', Status: 'Submitted' }, o)); };
  var get = function (t, k, id) { return must(db, t, k, id); };
  step('E upgrade', function () {
    eq('E1 tabs + setting added', upgradeIE(db).join(), 'Inspections tab,ExpenseClaims tab,Settings InspectionDays 7');
    eq('E2 second run changes nothing', upgradeIE(db).length, 0);
    eq('E3 columns', db.headers('Inspections').join(',') + ' | ' + db.headers('ExpenseClaims').join(','), IN_HEAD_.join(',') + ' | ' + CL_HEAD_.join(','));
    db.insert('Staff', [{ StaffID: 'STF-001', Name: 'Core Access office', Email: 'info@x', Status: 'Active' }, { StaffID: 'STF-002', Name: 'Rahim (site)', Status: 'Active' }]);
    testQuote_(db, 'CAE/Q/2026/001', 'Rental', [['RL-STD-20', 'Ringlock standard 2.0 m', 'pc', 10, 1, 100]]);
    job = quoteToJob(db, 'CAE/Q/2026/001', { date: '01-Nov-2026' });
    postOpeningStock(db, [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 10 }], '01-Nov-2026');
    postChallan(db, createChallan(db, { jobNo: job, date: '02-Nov-2026', lines: [{ itemCode: 'RL-STD-20', qty: 10 }] }));
    eq('E4 test job on hire', get('Jobs', 'JobNo', job).Status, 'On hire');
  });
  step('E inspections', function () {
    var due = inspDue(db, '13-Nov-2026');
    eq('E5 never inspected → due, days on hire', due.length + ' ' + due[0].jobNo + ' ' + due[0].last + '|' + due[0].days, '1 ' + job + ' |11');
    ins({ InspID: 'IN-a', InspDate: toDate('10-Nov-2026'), JobNo: job, Area: 'Tank east', Result: 'Safe' });
    eq('E6 inspected 3 days ago → not due', inspDue(db, '13-Nov-2026').length, 0);
    eq('E7 due again after 7 days', inspDue(db, '17-Nov-2026').map(function (x) { return x.last + ' ' + x.days; }).join(), '10-Nov-2026 7');
    ins({ InspID: 'IN-b', InspDate: toDate('12-Nov-2026'), StaffID: 'STF-002', JobNo: job, Kind: 'After bad weather', Area: 'Lift 3', Result: 'Unsafe', Findings: 'Two ties missing', Photo: 'p1.jpg', Photo2: 'p2.jpg' });
    var L = inspList(db), b = find(L, 'id', 'IN-b');
    eq('E8 list newest first, open flag, names', L[0].id + ' ' + b.open + ' ' + b.by + ' ' + b.photos + ' ' + find(L, 'id', 'IN-a').open + ' | ' + b.job, 'IN-b true Rahim (site) 2 false | ' + job + ' · Test Client · Tower A');
    throws('E9 safe one cannot be closed', function () { closeInspection(db, 'IN-a', 'x'); }, /only Unsafe/);
    throws('E10 close needs a note', function () { closeInspection(db, 'IN-b', ''); }, /note/);
    eq('E11 close', closeInspection(db, 'IN-b', 'Ties fixed 13 Nov', '13-Nov-2026'), 'IN-b closed.');
    eq('E12 closed date + note', ieDay_(get('Inspections', 'InspID', 'IN-b').ClosedDate) + ' ' + get('Inspections', 'InspID', 'IN-b').OfficeNote, '13-Nov-2026 Ties fixed 13 Nov');
    throws('E13 closed twice refused', function () { closeInspection(db, 'IN-b', 'again'); }, /already closed/);
    db.update('Jobs', get('Jobs', 'JobNo', job), { Status: 'Off-hire' });
    eq('E14 off-hire jobs never due', inspDue(db, '30-Nov-2026').length, 0);
    db.update('Jobs', get('Jobs', 'JobNo', job), { Status: 'On hire' });
  });
  step('E claims', function () {
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-a', JobNo: job, Details: 'Trip to site with ties' });
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-b', Category: 'Office', Payee: 'Stationers', Amount: 800 });
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-c', Category: 'Party' });
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-d', ReceiptPhoto: '' });
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-e', Amount: 0 });
    throws('E15 cash refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-a', { method: 'Cash' }); }, /no cash/);
    var r = approveClaim(db, 'CL-a', { method: 'Bank transfer', refNo: 'BT-77', by: 'STF-001' }), e = get('Expenses', 'ExpID', r.ref), c = get('ExpenseClaims', 'ClaimID', 'CL-a');
    eq('E16 expense row from the claim', [r.ref, fmtDay(dayNum(e.ExpDate)), e.Category, e.JobNo, e.Payee, e.Amount, e.Method, e.RefNo, e.ReceiptPhoto, e.ApprovedBy].join(' | '), 'EXP-00001 | 12-Nov-2026 | Transport | ' + job + ' | Truck owner | 3500 | Bank transfer | BT-77 | ExpenseClaims_Images/a.jpg | STF-001');
    eq('E17 expense note names the claim', e.Notes, 'Site claim CL-a by Core Access office: Trip to site with ties');
    eq('E18 claim approved + linked', c.Status + ' ' + c.ExpID, 'Approved EXP-00001');
    throws('E19 approved twice refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-a'); }, /is Approved \(EXP-00001\)/);
    eq('E20 office claim, default method', approveClaim(db, 'CL-b').ref + ' ' + get('Expenses', 'ExpID', 'EXP-00002').Method + ' ' + get('Expenses', 'ExpID', 'EXP-00002').JobNo + '|', 'EXP-00002 Bank transfer |');
    throws('E21 unknown category refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-c'); }, /category must be/);
    throws('E22 no photo refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-d'); }, /no receipt photo/);
    throws('E23 zero amount refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-e'); }, /more than 0/);
    eq('E24 refused claims made no expense', db.rows('Expenses').length, 2);
    throws('E25 reject needs a reason', function () { rejectClaim(db, 'CL-c', ''); }, /reason/);
    eq('E26 reject', rejectClaim(db, 'CL-c', 'Not a business cost') + ' ' + get('ExpenseClaims', 'ClaimID', 'CL-c').Status, 'CL-c rejected. Rejected');
    throws('E27 rejected cannot be approved', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-c'); }, /is Rejected/);
  });
  step('E panel', function () {
    var js = IE_HTML_.slice(IE_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, IE_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('E28 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    var used = {}, re = /act\('(\w+)'/g, m; while ((m = re.exec(IE_HTML_))) used[m[1]] = 1;
    eq('E29 every panel action exists on the server', Object.keys(used).sort().join(',') + ' ' + (Object.keys(used).filter(function (k) { return !IE_ACTIONS_[k]; }).join(',') || 'none'), 'approve,closeInsp,reject none');
    var init = ieClean_(ieInitDb_(db, '13-Nov-2026'));
    eq('E30 panel data', [init.open.length, init.due.length, init.recent.length, init.claims.map(function (x) { return x.id; }).join('+'), init.done.length, init.methods.join('/')].join(' '), '0 0 2 CL-e+CL-d 3 Bank transfer/Cheque/Pay order');
    eq('E31 panel data has no row numbers', JSON.stringify(init).indexOf('"_r"') < 0, true);
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}
