@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_Roles.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — app role Yard (add-on R1) · 13 Nov 2026
 * Tenth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_Roles › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq, Site, Roles.
 * Then Core Access › Tools › Upgrade sheet for inspections + claims (adds "Yard" to the Staff › AppRole dropdown; safe to repeat).
 *
 * App roles (Staff › AppRole, matched by Email): Admin · Field · Yard · Viewer.
 *   Yard = stock, challans (Delivery / Sale / Return), cut · repair · scrap, damage photos. No quote requests,
 *   inspections or expense claims (AppSheet hides those screens; the office panels refuse them too).
 * Replaces by name: appRoleCheck_ (Sets). Wraps: upgradeIE, quoteFromRequest, approveClaim, selfTestAllParts_.
 */
var CAE_R1_VERSION = 'R1 · 13 Nov 2026';
APP_ROLES_ = ['Admin', 'Viewer', 'Field', 'Yard'];
var R1_STOCK_ROLES_ = ['Admin', 'Field', 'Yard']; // challans + conversions (swept)
var R1_SITE_ROLES_ = ['Admin', 'Field'];          // quote requests, inspections, expense claims

// Swept app records (challans, conversions): Admin, Field or Yard.
function appRoleCheck_(db, email, allowed) {
  if (S(db, 'AppCheckRole', 'No') !== 'Yes') return;
  allowed = allowed || R1_STOCK_ROLES_;
  if (db.headers('Staff').indexOf('AppRole') < 0) throw new Error('Staff › AppRole is missing — run Upgrade sheet for O7');
  var e = String(email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  var s = e && db.rows('Staff').filter(function (x) { return String(x.Email || '').trim().toLowerCase() === e && x.Status !== 'Inactive'; })[0];
  if (!s) throw new Error((email || 'Unknown user') + ' is not in the Staff tab (Email) — ask the office to add you');
  if (allowed.indexOf(String(s.AppRole)) < 0) throw new Error(email + ' has app role ' + (s.AppRole || 'none') + ' — only ' + allowed.slice(1).join(', ') + ' or Admin can submit');
}
// Office panels: a request / claim made by a Staff member whose role may not send it is refused (Drop / Reject still work).
function r1SiteRole_(db, id, staffId, what) {
  if (S(db, 'AppCheckRole', 'No') !== 'Yes' || blank(staffId)) return;
  var s = find(db.rows('Staff'), 'StaffID', staffId);
  if (s && R1_SITE_ROLES_.indexOf(String(s.AppRole)) < 0) throw new Error(id + ': ' + (s.Name || staffId) + ' has app role ' + (s.AppRole || 'none') + ' — ' + what + ' only from Field or Admin. Use ' + (what === 'expense claims' ? 'Reject' : 'Drop') + '.');
}
if (typeof quoteFromRequest === 'function') {
  var R1_qfr0_ = quoteFromRequest;
  quoteFromRequest = function (db, id, o) { r1SiteRole_(db, id, must(db, 'QuoteReqs', 'ReqID', id).StaffID, 'quote requests'); return R1_qfr0_(db, id, o); };
}
if (typeof approveClaim === 'function') {
  var R1_appr0_ = approveClaim;
  approveClaim = function (db, id, o) { r1SiteRole_(db, id, must(db, 'ExpenseClaims', 'ClaimID', id).StaffID, 'expense claims'); return R1_appr0_(db, id, o); };
}
if (typeof upgradeIE === 'function') {
  var R1_upIE0_ = upgradeIE;
  upgradeIE = function (db) {
    var d = R1_upIE0_(db);
    if (db.ss && db.hasTab('Staff') && db.headers('Staff').indexOf('AppRole') >= 0) {
      var a = ensureAllowed_(db, 'Staff', 'AppRole', ['Yard']);
      if (a.length) { d.push('Staff.AppRole dropdown + Yard'); log_(db, 'Upgrade R1', '', 'Staff.AppRole + Yard'); }
    }
    return d;
  };
}

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var R1_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;
  selfTestAllParts_ = function () { return R1_parts0_().concat([['R1', selfTestR1()]]); };
}
function selfTestR1() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws;
  var db = testSeed_('Full period'), t0 = dayNum(today()), D = function (k) { return fromDay(t0 - k); }, yr = String(D(0).getFullYear()), JOB = 'CAE/J/2026/001';
  upgradeO6(db); upgradeO7(db); upgradeQR(db); upgradeIE(db);
  db.insert('Staff', [{ StaffID: 'STF-Y', Name: 'Yard', Email: 'Yard@Test', AppRole: 'Yard', Status: 'Active' }, { StaffID: 'STF-F', Name: 'Field', Email: 'field@test', AppRole: 'Field', Status: 'Active' },
    { StaffID: 'STF-V', Name: 'Viewer', Email: 'viewer@test', AppRole: 'Viewer', Status: 'Active' }]);
  step('Y roles', function () {
    eq('Y1 roles list', APP_ROLES_.join(','), 'Admin,Viewer,Field,Yard');
    eq('Y2 Yard may submit stock records', (function () { try { appRoleCheck_(db, 'yard@test'); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    throws('Y3 Viewer still refused, message names Yard', function () { appRoleCheck_(db, 'viewer@test'); }, /only Field, Yard or Admin can submit/);
  });
  step('Y sweep', function () {
    db.insert('Jobs', { JobNo: JOB, CustID: 'CUST-0001', SiteID: 'SITE-0001', JobType: 'Rental', HireBasis: 'Item', Status: 'Awaiting order' });
    postOpeningStock(db, [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 5 }], D(10));
    db.insert('Challans', { ChallanNo: 'APP-y0000001', Kind: 'Delivery', ChallanDate: D(1), JobNo: JOB, Status: 'Submitted', SubmittedBy: 'yard@test' });
    db.insert('ChallanLines', { LineID: 'APP-y01', ChallanNo: 'APP-y0000001', ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 2, QtyDamaged: 0, QtyLost: 0 });
    var r = appSweepRun_({ db: db, noPdf: true });
    eq('Y4 Yard challan posted by the sweep', r.posted + ' ' + (find(db.rows('Challans'), 'ChallanNo', 'CAE/DC/' + yr + '/001') || {}).Status, '1 Posted');
  });
  step('Y office panels', function () {
    var add = function (o) { db.insert('QuoteReqs', Object.assign({ ReqDate: toDate('13-Nov-2026'), Status: 'New', CustID: 'CUST-0001', WorkType: 'Rental', Details: 'Ringlock' }, o)); };
    add({ ReqID: 'QR-y1', StaffID: 'STF-Y' }); add({ ReqID: 'QR-f1', StaffID: 'STF-F' }); add({ ReqID: 'QR-o1' });
    throws('Y5 request from Yard staff refused', function () { quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-y1'); }, /app role Yard — quote requests only from Field or Admin/);
    eq('Y6 Field + office requests still quoted', [quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-f1', { date: '2026-11-13' }).ref, quoteFromRequest(db, 'QR-o1', { date: '2026-11-13' }).ref].join(' '), 'CAE/Q/2026/001 CAE/Q/2026/002');
    eq('Y7 Yard request can be dropped', dropRequest(db, 'QR-y1', 'yard'), 'QR-y1 dropped.');
    var cl = function (o) { db.insert('ExpenseClaims', Object.assign({ ClaimDate: toDate('12-Nov-2026'), Category: 'Transport', Payee: 'Truck', Amount: 100, ReceiptPhoto: 'a.jpg', Status: 'Submitted' }, o)); };
    cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-y', StaffID: 'STF-Y' }); cl({ ClaimID: 'CL-f', StaffID: 'STF-F' });
    throws('Y8 claim from Yard staff refused', function () { approveClaim(db, 'CL-y'); }, /expense claims only from Field or Admin/);
    eq('Y9 Field claim approved, Yard claim rejected', approveClaim(db, 'CL-f').ref + ' ' + rejectClaim(db, 'CL-y', 'yard'), 'EXP-00001 CL-y rejected.');
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_Terms.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — terms per quote (add-on QT1) · 15 Nov 2026
 * Eleventh script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_Terms › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Core, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq, Site, Roles, Terms. No menu change.
 * Use: Core Access › Quotes › pick a quote › "Terms". After saving a quote the panel opens its Terms screen by itself.
 * First use: the Terms screen offers "Set up now" (one time): 5 columns at the END of Quotes + a QuoteTerms tab.
 *
 * Per quote, blank = standard (Settings / customer):
 *   Advance %, minimum hire months (same Quotes columns as the quote form) · PaymentDays · ValidityDays ·
 *   LateInterestPct · NoticeDays · PartPeriodRule. Clauses: keep / change wording / remove each standard clause,
 *   add extra clauses (into a section, or a new "Special Conditions" section at the end). QuoteTerms is written only
 *   by the panel (tab protected with a warning).
 * The values reach everything that depends on them, so the quote and the money agree:
 *   quote PDF ({{tokens}}, Valid until, clauses) · Quotes.ValidUntil formula + expiry (Refresh statuses) ·
 *   Invoices.DueDate formula + overdue + invoice PDF wording (payment days, late interest) for the job's invoices ·
 *   hire end of m² / m³ / set / lump-sum jobs (notice days) · hire billing part-month rule for the job.
 * Wraps: quoteTokens, termsFor, PDF_SPECS.Quote, PDF_SPECS.Invoice, hireEndDay, billJob_, resolveF, uiOffHire_,
 *   uiGetDb_ (quote → terms summary), selfTestAllParts_. Replaces: refreshStatuses (same + per-quote days).
 *   CALC: Quotes.ValidUntil, Invoices.DueDate (old formulas kept until the set-up has run).
 */
var CAE_QT_VERSION = 'QT1 · 15 Nov 2026';
var QT_COLS_ = ['ValidityDays', 'PaymentDays', 'NoticeDays', 'LateInterestPct', 'PartPeriodRule'];
var QT_HEAD_ = ['QuoteNo', 'ClauseID', 'Action', 'Section', 'SectionTitle', 'ClauseNo', 'Title', 'Text', 'SortOrder', 'UpdatedBy', 'UpdatedAt'];
var QT_RULES_ = ['Full period', 'Pro rata'];
var QT_SPECIAL_ = 'Special Conditions';

function qtReady_(db) { return db.hasTab('QuoteTerms') && QT_COLS_.every(function (c) { return db.headers('Quotes').indexOf(c) >= 0; }); }
function qtQuote_(db, no) { return blank(no) ? null : find(db.rows('Quotes'), 'QuoteNo', no); }
function qtJobQuote_(db, job) { return job ? qtQuote_(db, job.QuoteNo) : null; }
function qtHas_(q, k) { return !!q && (k in q) && !blank(q[k]); }
function qtRows_(db, no) { return db.hasTab('QuoteTerms') ? db.rows('QuoteTerms').filter(function (r) { return String(r.QuoteNo) === String(no); }) : []; }
function qtPh_(s) { return /\[(?!to fill in Settings\]|verify with accountant\])[^\]]*\]/.test(String(s)); }
function qtHire_(type) { return type === 'Rental' || type === 'Project'; }
function qtRuleTxt_(r) { return r === 'Pro rata' ? 'a part month is charged pro rata by days' : 'a part month is charged as a full month'; }

// ======================================================================= set-up (one time, safe to repeat)
function upgradeQT(db) {
  var done = [];
  QT_COLS_.forEach(function (c) { if (db.ensureColumn('Quotes', c)) done.push('Quotes + ' + c); });
  if (db.ensureTab('QuoteTerms', QT_HEAD_, [])) {
    done.push('QuoteTerms tab');
    if (db.ss) {
      var t = db.sheet('QuoteTerms');
      t.getRange(2, QT_HEAD_.indexOf('UpdatedAt') + 1, 999, 1).setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy hh:mm');
      try { t.protect().setDescription('Written by the panel: Quotes › Terms. Change terms there, not here.').setWarningOnly(true); } catch (e) { /* protection is a convenience */ }
    }
  }
  if (db.ss && done.length) {
    var s = db.sheet('Quotes'), h = db.headers('Quotes'), n = Math.max(s.getMaxRows() - 1, 1);
    s.getRange(2, h.indexOf('PartPeriodRule') + 1, n, 1).setDataValidation(SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(QT_RULES_, true).setAllowInvalid(false).build());
    s.getRange(2, h.indexOf('LateInterestPct') + 1, n, 1).setNumberFormat('0.0%');
    db.fillFormulas('Quotes'); db.fillFormulas('Invoices');
  }
  log_(db, 'Upgrade QT', '', done.join('; ') || 'up to date');
  return done;
}

// ======================================================================= formulas: per-quote validity + payment days
var QT_VALID_OLD_ = CALC.Quotes.ValidUntil;
var QT_VALID_NEW_ = '=IF({QuoteDate}="","",{QuoteDate}+IF(N({ValidityDays})>0,{ValidityDays},INDEX([Settings.Value],MATCH("QuoteValidityDays",[Settings.Key],0))))';
var QT_DUE_OLD_ = CALC.Invoices.DueDate;
var QT_DUE_NEW_ = '=IF({InvDate}="","",{InvDate}+IFERROR(1/(1/INDEX([Quotes.PaymentDays],MATCH(INDEX([Jobs.QuoteNo],MATCH({JobNo},[Jobs.JobNo],0)),[Quotes.QuoteNo],0))),' +
  'IFERROR(1*INDEX([Customers.PaymentDays],MATCH({CustID},[Customers.CustID],0)),INDEX([Settings.Value],MATCH("PaymentDaysDefault",[Settings.Key],0))*1)))';
CALC.Quotes.ValidUntil = QT_VALID_NEW_;
CALC.Invoices.DueDate = QT_DUE_NEW_;
var QT_resolveF0_ = resolveF;
resolveF = function (db, t, tpl, r) {
  if ((tpl === QT_VALID_NEW_ || tpl === QT_DUE_NEW_) && db.headers('Quotes').indexOf('ValidityDays') < 0) tpl = tpl === QT_VALID_NEW_ ? QT_VALID_OLD_ : QT_DUE_OLD_;
  return QT_resolveF0_(db, t, tpl, r);
};

// ======================================================================= quote PDF: tokens + clauses
var QT_CUR_ = null; // quote whose PDF is being built (clause overrides apply only then)
var QT_tokens0_ = quoteTokens;
quoteTokens = function (db, q) {
  var t = QT_tokens0_(db, q);
  if (qtHas_(q, 'PaymentDays')) t.PaymentDays = String(num(q.PaymentDays));
  if (qtHas_(q, 'ValidityDays')) t.ValidityDays = String(num(q.ValidityDays));
  if (qtHas_(q, 'NoticeDays')) t.NoticeDays = String(num(q.NoticeDays));
  if (qtHas_(q, 'LateInterestPct')) t.LateInterest = pctTxt(q.LateInterestPct);
  if (qtHas_(q, 'PartPeriodRule')) t.PartPeriod = qtRuleTxt_(String(q.PartPeriodRule));
  return t;
};
function qtApply_(base, ov) {
  if (!ov.length) return base;
  var by = {}, out = [];
  ov.forEach(function (o) { if (o.Action !== 'Add' && !blank(o.ClauseID)) by[o.ClauseID] = o; });
  base.forEach(function (r) {
    var o = by[r.ClauseID];
    if (o && o.Action === 'Remove') return;
    out.push(o && o.Action === 'Replace' ? Object.assign({}, r, { Title: blank(o.Title) ? r.Title : o.Title, Text: o.Text }) : r);
  });
  ov.filter(function (o) { return o.Action === 'Add'; }).forEach(function (o) {
    out.push({ ClauseID: '', QuoteType: '', Section: o.Section, SectionTitle: o.SectionTitle, ClauseNo: o.ClauseNo, Title: o.Title, Text: o.Text, SortOrder: num(o.SortOrder), Active: 'Yes' });
  });
  return out.sort(function (a, b) { return num(a.SortOrder) - num(b.SortOrder); });
}
var QT_termsFor0_ = termsFor;
termsFor = function (db, type) {
  var base = QT_termsFor0_(db, type);
  return QT_CUR_ && type !== 'PO' ? qtApply_(base, qtRows_(db, QT_CUR_)) : base;
};
function qtSetPair_(spec, label, val) {
  spec.blocks.forEach(function (b) { if (b.t === 'pairs') b.rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r[0] === label) r[1] = val; if (r[2] === label) r[3] = val; }); });
}
var QT_specQuote0_ = PDF_SPECS.Quote;
PDF_SPECS.Quote = function (db, no) {
  var prev = QT_CUR_; QT_CUR_ = no;
  try {
    var spec = QT_specQuote0_(db, no), q = qtQuote_(db, no);
    if (qtHas_(q, 'ValidityDays') && dayNum(q.QuoteDate) != null) qtSetPair_(spec, 'Valid until', longDate(fromDay(dayNum(q.QuoteDate) + num(q.ValidityDays))));
    return spec;
  } finally { QT_CUR_ = prev; }
};
// Invoice PDF: payment days + late interest of the job's quote.
var QT_specInv0_ = PDF_SPECS.Invoice;
PDF_SPECS.Invoice = function (db, invNo) {
  var spec = QT_specInv0_(db, invNo), inv = find(db.rows('Invoices'), 'InvNo', invNo) || {};
  var q = qtJobQuote_(db, find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'JobNo', inv.JobNo));
  var pd = qtHas_(q, 'PaymentDays') ? num(q.PaymentDays) : null, li = qtHas_(q, 'LateInterestPct') ? pctTxt(q.LateInterestPct) : null;
  if (pd == null && !li) return spec;
  spec.blocks.forEach(function (b) {
    if (b.t !== 'p' || typeof b.text !== 'string') return;
    if (pd != null) b.text = b.text.replace(/Payable within \d+ days/, 'Payable within ' + pd + ' days');
    if (li) b.text = b.text.replace(/interest at [\d.]+% per month/, 'interest at ' + li + ' per month');
  });
  if (pd != null && blank(inv.DueDate) && dayNum(inv.InvDate) != null) qtSetPair_(spec, 'Due date', longDate(fromDay(dayNum(inv.InvDate) + pd)));
  return spec;
};

// ======================================================================= billing + statuses
var QT_hireEnd0_ = hireEndDay;
hireEndDay = function (db, job) {
  var q = job.HireBasis === 'Item' ? null : qtJobQuote_(db, job);
  if (!qtHas_(q, 'NoticeDays')) return QT_hireEnd0_(db, job);
  var oh = db.rows('OffHire').filter(function (x) { return x.JobNo === job.JobNo && x.Scope === 'Full'; }), nd = num(q.NoticeDays);
  if (!oh.length) return null;
  return Math.max.apply(null, oh.map(function (x) { return Math.max(dayNum(x.OffHireDate) || 0, (dayNum(x.NoticeDate) || 0) + nd); }));
};
var QT_billJob0_ = billJob_;
billJob_ = function (db, job, asOf, P, rule) {
  var q = qtJobQuote_(db, job);
  return QT_billJob0_(db, job, asOf, P, qtHas_(q, 'PartPeriodRule') ? String(q.PartPeriodRule) : rule);
};
var QT_offHire0_ = uiOffHire_;
uiOffHire_ = function (db, a) {
  var r = QT_offHire0_(db, a), q = qtJobQuote_(db, find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'JobNo', a.jobNo));
  if (r && r.msg && qtHas_(q, 'NoticeDays')) r.msg = r.msg.replace(/notice \+ \d+ days/, 'notice + ' + num(q.NoticeDays) + ' days');
  return r;
};
function refreshStatuses(db, asOf) {
  var d = dayNum(asOf || today()), vd = num(S(db, 'QuoteValidityDays', 30)), pd = num(S(db, 'PaymentDaysDefault', 7)), c = 0;
  db.rows('Quotes').forEach(function (q) {
    if (['Draft', 'Sent'].indexOf(q.Status) < 0 || dayNum(q.QuoteDate) == null) return;
    if (dayNum(q.QuoteDate) + (qtHas_(q, 'ValidityDays') ? num(q.ValidityDays) : vd) < d) { db.update('Quotes', q, { Status: 'Expired' }); c++; }
  });
  db.rows('Proformas').forEach(function (p) { if (!blank(p.PINo)) refreshDoc(db, 'PI', p.PINo); });
  db.rows('Bills').forEach(function (b) { if (!blank(b.BillID)) refreshDoc(db, 'Bill', b.BillID); });
  db.rows('Invoices').forEach(function (i) {
    if (blank(i.InvNo)) return;
    var st = refreshDoc(db, 'INV', i.InvNo), cu = find(db.rows('Customers'), 'CustID', i.CustID), q = qtJobQuote_(db, find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'JobNo', i.JobNo));
    var days = qtHas_(q, 'PaymentDays') ? num(q.PaymentDays) : (cu && !blank(cu.PaymentDays) ? num(cu.PaymentDays) : pd);
    if (['Sent', 'Part paid'].indexOf(st) >= 0 && dayNum(i.InvDate) + days < d && docInfo(db, 'INV', i.InvNo).balance > 0.005) { db.update('Invoices', i, { Status: 'Overdue' }); c++; }
  });
  return c;
}

// ======================================================================= Terms screen: data + save
function qtStd_(db, q) {
  var cu = find(db.rows('Customers'), 'CustID', q.CustID) || {}, own = !blank(cu.PaymentDays);
  return {
    AdvancePct: [num(S(db, 'AdvancePct', 0.5)), 'company default'], MinMonths: [num(S(db, 'MinHireMonths', 1)), 'company default'],
    PaymentDays: [own ? num(cu.PaymentDays) : num(S(db, 'PaymentDaysDefault', 7)), own ? 'customer ' + (cu.Name || q.CustID) : 'company default'],
    ValidityDays: [num(S(db, 'QuoteValidityDays', 30)), 'company default'], LateInterestPct: [num(S(db, 'LateInterestPctMonth', 0.02)), 'company default'],
    NoticeDays: [num(S(db, 'OffHireNoticeDays', 7)), 'company default'], PartPeriodRule: [String(S(db, 'PartPeriodRule', 'Full period')), 'company default'],
  };
}
// [key, label, unit, kind] — kind: pct (stored 0–1, shown 0–100), int, rule
var QT_FIELDS_ = [['AdvancePct', 'Advance with the order', '%', 'pct'], ['PaymentDays', 'Invoices payable within', 'days', 'int'], ['ValidityDays', 'Quotation valid for', 'days', 'int'],
  ['LateInterestPct', 'Late payment interest', '% a month', 'pct'], ['MinMonths', 'Minimum hire', 'months', 'int', 1], ['NoticeDays', 'Off-hire notice', 'days', 'int', 1], ['PartPeriodRule', 'Part month', '', 'rule', 1]];
function qtFieldsFor_(type) { return QT_FIELDS_.filter(function (f) { return !f[4] || qtHire_(type); }); }
function qtShow_(f, v) { return f[3] === 'pct' ? r2(num(v) * 100) : f[3] === 'rule' ? String(v) : num(v); }
function qtStdTxt_(f, v) { return f[3] === 'rule' ? (v === 'Pro rata' ? 'pro rata by days' : 'full month') : qtShow_(f, v) + (f[2] === '%' || f[2] === '% a month' ? f[2] : ' ' + f[2]); }
// value set for this quote, or '' when it is the standard
function qtCustom_(f, q, std) {
  var k = f[0], v = q[k];
  if (k === 'AdvancePct' || k === 'MinMonths') return blank(v) || Math.abs(num(v) - num(std[k][0])) < 1e-9 ? '' : qtShow_(f, v);
  return blank(v) ? '' : qtShow_(f, v);
}
function qtSections_(base, added) {
  var out = [], seen = {}, max = 0;
  base.forEach(function (r) { var s = String(r.Section); if (!seen[s]) { seen[s] = 1; out.push({ no: s, title: r.SectionTitle }); } if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) max = Math.max(max, num(s)); });
  return { list: out.concat([{ no: String(max + 1), title: QT_SPECIAL_ + ' (new section at the end)' }]), special: String(max + 1), seen: seen };
}
function qtSummary_(db, q) {
  if (!q) return '';
  var prev = QT_CUR_, ph = 0, n = 0;
  QT_CUR_ = q.QuoteNo;
  try { termsFor(db, q.QuoteType || 'Rental').forEach(function (t) { if (qtPh_(t.Text)) ph++; }); } finally { QT_CUR_ = prev; }
  if (qtReady_(db)) {
    var std = qtStd_(db, q);
    qtFieldsFor_(q.QuoteType || 'Rental').forEach(function (f) { if (qtCustom_(f, q, std) !== '') n++; });
    n += qtRows_(db, q.QuoteNo).length;
  }
  return (n ? n + ' change' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') : 'standard') + (ph ? ' · ' + ph + ' to fill' : '');
}
function qtLock_(db, q) {
  var job = find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'QuoteNo', q.QuoteNo);
  if (job) return q.QuoteNo + ' already has job ' + job.JobNo + ' — the terms are fixed. Change them by a variation on the job.';
  if (['Draft', 'Sent'].indexOf(q.Status) < 0) return q.QuoteNo + ' is ' + q.Status + ' — use Revise on the quote to reopen it.';
  return '';
}
function qtView_(db, no) {
  var q = must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', no), type = q.QuoteType || 'Rental', cu = find(db.rows('Customers'), 'CustID', q.CustID) || {};
  var std = qtStd_(db, q), ready = qtReady_(db), ov = qtRows_(db, no), base = QT_termsFor0_(db, type), by = {};
  ov.forEach(function (o) { if (o.Action !== 'Add') by[o.ClauseID] = o; });
  var sec = qtSections_(base, ov), lock = qtLock_(db, q);
  return {
    no: no, rev: num(q.Rev), type: type, status: q.Status, custName: cu.Name || q.CustID, ready: ready, locked: !!lock, lockMsg: lock, tokens: quoteTokens(db, q),
    fields: qtFieldsFor_(type).map(function (f) { return { key: f[0], label: f[1], unit: f[2], kind: f[3], value: ready || f[0] === 'AdvancePct' || f[0] === 'MinMonths' ? qtCustom_(f, q, std) : '', stdTxt: qtStdTxt_(f, std[f[0]][0]), src: std[f[0]][1] }; }),
    clauses: base.map(function (r) {
      var o = by[r.ClauseID], mode = !o ? 'std' : o.Action === 'Remove' ? 'remove' : 'edit', eff = mode === 'edit' ? o.Text : r.Text;
      return { id: r.ClauseID, sec: String(r.Section), secTitle: r.SectionTitle, no: String(r.ClauseNo), title: r.Title, std: String(r.Text), mode: mode, text: mode === 'edit' ? String(o.Text) : '', ph: mode !== 'remove' && qtPh_(eff) };
    }),
    added: ov.filter(function (o) { return o.Action === 'Add'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return num(a.SortOrder) - num(b.SortOrder); })
      .map(function (o) { return { sec: sec.seen[String(o.Section)] ? String(o.Section) : sec.special, no: String(o.ClauseNo), title: o.Title, text: String(o.Text) }; }),
    sections: sec.list,
  };
}
function uiTermsGet(no) { return uiRun_(function (db) { return qtView_(db, no); }, true); }
function qtNum_(v, kind, lo, hi, what) {
  if (blank(v) || String(v).trim() === '') return '';
  var n = num(String(v).replace('%', ''));
  if (!(n >= lo && n <= hi)) throw new Error(what + ' must be from ' + lo + ' to ' + hi);
  if (kind === 'int' && Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) > 1e-9) throw new Error(what + ' must be a whole number');
  return kind === 'pct' ? r2(n) / 100 : n;
}
// a = { quoteNo, settings: { key: value shown in the panel ('' = standard) }, clauses: [{ id, mode: std|edit|remove, text }], added: [{ section, title, text }] }
function qtSave_(db, a) {
  var q = must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', a.quoteNo), lock = qtLock_(db, q);
  if (lock) throw new Error(lock);
  if (!qtReady_(db)) { upgradeQT(db); q = must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', a.quoteNo); }
  var type = q.QuoteType || 'Rental', std = qtStd_(db, q), s = a.settings || {}, p = {}, n = 0;
  var lim = { AdvancePct: [0, 100, 'Advance %'], PaymentDays: [1, 365, 'Payment days'], ValidityDays: [1, 365, 'Validity days'], LateInterestPct: [0, 10, 'Late interest % a month'], MinMonths: [1, 60, 'Minimum hire months'], NoticeDays: [0, 90, 'Off-hire notice days'] };
  qtFieldsFor_(type).forEach(function (f) {
    var k = f[0], v;
    if (f[3] === 'rule') { v = String(s[k] || ''); if (v && QT_RULES_.indexOf(v) < 0) throw new Error('Part month must be Full period or Pro rata'); if (v === std[k][0]) v = ''; }
    else { v = qtNum_(s[k], f[3], lim[k][0], lim[k][1], lim[k][2]); if (v !== '' && Math.abs(v - num(std[k][0])) < 1e-9) v = ''; }
    if (k === 'AdvancePct' || k === 'MinMonths') { p[k] = v === '' ? num(std[k][0]) : v; if (v !== '') n++; } else { p[k] = v; if (v !== '') n++; }
  });
  var base = QT_termsFor0_(db, type), byId = {}, rows = [];
  base.forEach(function (r) { byId[r.ClauseID] = r; });
  (a.clauses || []).forEach(function (c) {
    var b = byId[c.id]; if (!b) throw new Error('Clause ' + c.id + ' is not in the ' + type + ' terms');
    var r = { Section: b.Section, SectionTitle: b.SectionTitle, ClauseNo: b.ClauseNo, Title: b.Title, SortOrder: num(b.SortOrder), ClauseID: b.ClauseID };
    if (c.mode === 'remove') rows.push(Object.assign(r, { Action: 'Remove', Text: '' }));
    else if (c.mode === 'edit') {
      var t = String(c.text || '').trim();
      if (!t) throw new Error('Clause ' + b.ClauseNo + ': the wording is empty — choose Remove instead');
      if (t !== String(b.Text).trim()) rows.push(Object.assign(r, { Action: 'Replace', Text: t }));
    }
  });
  var sec = qtSections_(base, []), maxSort = base.reduce(function (m, r) { return Math.max(m, num(r.SortOrder)); }, 0), cnt = {};
  (a.added || []).forEach(function (x, i) {
    var t = String(x.text || '').trim(); if (!t) throw new Error('Extra clause ' + (i + 1) + ': the wording is empty');
    var sn = sec.seen[String(x.section)] ? String(x.section) : sec.special, inSec = base.filter(function (r) { return String(r.Section) === sn; });
    var last = inSec.reduce(function (m, r) { var p2 = String(r.ClauseNo).split('.'); return p2.length === 2 && p2[0] === sn ? Math.max(m, num(p2[1])) : m; }, 0);
    cnt[sn] = (cnt[sn] || 0) + 1;
    var so = inSec.length ? inSec.reduce(function (m, r) { return Math.max(m, num(r.SortOrder)); }, 0) : maxSort + 1;
    rows.push({ ClauseID: '', Action: 'Add', Section: sn, SectionTitle: inSec.length ? inSec[0].SectionTitle : QT_SPECIAL_, ClauseNo: (sn ? sn + '.' : '') + (last + cnt[sn]), Title: String(x.title || '').trim(), Text: t, SortOrder: r2(so + cnt[sn] / 100) });
  });
  // everything checked — now write (quote row, then this quote's clause rows)
  uiPutRow_(db, 'Quotes', q, p);
  uiDelRows_(db, 'QuoteTerms', qtRows_(db, q.QuoteNo));
  var who = user_(), now = new Date();
  if (rows.length) db.insert('QuoteTerms', rows.map(function (r) { r.QuoteNo = q.QuoteNo; r.UpdatedBy = who; r.UpdatedAt = now; return r; }));
  n += rows.length;
  log_(db, 'Quote terms', q.QuoteNo, n ? n + ' change(s) from standard' : 'all standard');
  var sum2 = qtSummary_(db, must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', q.QuoteNo));
  return { msg: q.QuoteNo + ' terms saved: ' + sum2 + '. Make the quotation PDF to see them.', ref: q.QuoteNo, warn: / to fill/.test(sum2) };
}
UI_ACTIONS_.saveTerms = function (db, a) { return qtSave_(db, a); };
UI_ACTIONS_.setupTerms = function (db) { var d = upgradeQT(db); return d.length ? 'Set up: ' + d.join(', ') + '.' : 'Already set up.'; };
var QT_uiGet0_ = uiGetDb_;
uiGetDb_ = function (db, kind, a) {
  var r = QT_uiGet0_(db, kind, a);
  if (kind === 'quote' && r && r.header) r.termsInfo = qtSummary_(db, r.header);
  return r;
};

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var QT_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;
  selfTestAllParts_ = function () { return QT_parts0_().concat([['QT', selfTestQT()]]); };

function selfTestQT() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws;
  var db = pdfTestDb_(), Q1 = 'CAE/Q/2026/001', Q2 = 'CAE/Q/2026/002', Q3 = 'CAE/Q/2026/003', Q4 = 'CAE/Q/2026/004';
  var save = function (no, a) { return uiDoDb_(db, 'saveTerms', Object.assign({ quoteNo: no, settings: {}, clauses: [], added: [] }, a)); };
  var q = function (no) { return must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', no); };
  var txt = function (no) { return JSON.stringify(PDF_SPECS.Quote(db, no).blocks); };
  var cl = function (type, no) { return find(QT_termsFor0_(db, type), 'ClauseNo', no); };
  step('T set-up', function () {
    upgradeO3(db, { docBaseId: 'X' });
    eq('T1 panel screen injected (4 places)', QT_INJECTED_, 4);
    eq('T2 not set up yet: view still works', qtView_(db, Q1).ready, false);
    eq('T3 set-up: 5 columns + tab', upgradeQT(db).length, 6);
    eq('T4 second run changes nothing', upgradeQT(db).length, 0);
    eq('T5 summary before any change (test quote has 2 months minimum)', qtSummary_(db, q(Q1)), '1 change · 1 to fill');
    var v = qtView_(db, Q1);
    eq('T6 Rental fields incl. hire terms', v.fields.map(function (f) { return f.key; }).join(','), 'AdvancePct,PaymentDays,ValidityDays,LateInterestPct,MinMonths,NoticeDays,PartPeriodRule');
    eq('T7 Sale fields: no hire terms', qtView_(db, Q2).fields.length, 4);
    eq('T8 standard shown from the customer', find(v.fields, 'key', 'PaymentDays').src + ' ' + find(v.fields, 'key', 'PaymentDays').stdTxt, 'customer Test Client 7 days');
  });
  step('T save + validation', function () {
    throws('T9 payment days 0 refused', function () { save(Q1, { settings: { PaymentDays: '0' } }); }, /from 1 to 365/);
    throws('T10 late interest 20% refused', function () { save(Q1, { settings: { LateInterestPct: '20' } }); }, /from 0 to 10/);
    throws('T11 part days refused', function () { save(Q1, { settings: { NoticeDays: '2.5' } }); }, /whole number/);
    throws('T12 unknown rule refused', function () { save(Q1, { settings: { PartPeriodRule: 'Weekly' } }); }, /Full period or Pro rata/);
    throws('T13 empty changed wording refused', function () { save(Q1, { settings: { MinMonths: '9' }, clauses: [{ id: cl('Rental', '3.3').ClauseID, mode: 'edit', text: ' ' }] }); }, /choose Remove/);
    eq('T13b a refused save writes nothing', q(Q1).MinMonths, 2);
    throws('T14 clause of another type refused', function () { save(Q1, { clauses: [{ id: cl('Sale', '5.4').ClauseID, mode: 'remove' }] }); }, /not in the Rental terms/);
    var r = save(Q1, {
      settings: { AdvancePct: '30', PaymentDays: '30', ValidityDays: '15', LateInterestPct: '3', MinMonths: '2', NoticeDays: '14', PartPeriodRule: 'Pro rata' },
      clauses: [{ id: cl('Rental', '3.3').ClauseID, mode: 'edit', text: 'The client shall give {{NoticeDays}} days written notice by email to off-hire.' },
        { id: cl('Rental', '7.1').ClauseID, mode: 'remove' }, { id: cl('Rental', '4.2').ClauseID, mode: 'edit', text: cl('Rental', '4.2').Text }],
      added: [{ section: '4', title: 'Retention', text: '10% of each invoice is retained until handover.' }, { section: '_new', title: 'Access', text: 'Site access road to be kept clear by the client.' }],
    });
    var x = q(Q1);
    eq('T15 saved on the quote', [x.AdvancePct, x.PaymentDays, x.ValidityDays, x.LateInterestPct, x.MinMonths, x.NoticeDays, x.PartPeriodRule].join(' '), '0.3 30 15 0.03 2 14 Pro rata');
    eq('T16 clause rows (same wording is not a change)', qtRows_(db, Q1).map(function (o) { return o.Action + ' ' + o.ClauseNo; }).join(', '), 'Replace 3.3, Remove 7.1, Add 4.5, Add 8.1');
    eq('T17 summary', r.msg, Q1 + ' terms saved: 11 changes · 1 to fill. Make the quotation PDF to see them.');
    save(Q2, { settings: { PaymentDays: '7', ValidityDays: '30' } });
    eq('T18 values equal to the standard stay standard', qtSummary_(db, q(Q2)) + ' ' + q(Q2).PaymentDays + '|' + q(Q2).ValidityDays, 'standard · 5 to fill |');
  });
  step('T quote PDF', function () {
    var t = txt(Q1);
    eq('T19 advance + payment days + interest', /30% advance with the work order/.test(t) && /within 30 days of the invoice date/.test(t) && /interest at 3% per month/.test(t), true);
    eq('T20 part month pro rata in 3.2', /a part month is charged pro rata by days/.test(t), true);
    eq('T21 changed 3.3 with token filled', /give 14 days written notice by email/.test(t), true);
    eq('T22 7.1 removed, valid until = 20 Nov + 15', !/This quotation is valid for/.test(t) && /5 December 2026/.test(t), true);
    eq('T23 extra clause in section 4 + special section 8, acceptance 9', /"4\.5","Retention"/.test(t) && /8\.  SPECIAL CONDITIONS/.test(t) && /9\.  ACCEPTANCE/.test(t), true);
    eq('T24 other quotes untouched', /Retention|SPECIAL CONDITIONS/.test(txt(Q2)), false);
    eq('T25 standard terms outside a quote unchanged', termsFor(db, 'Rental').length, QT_termsFor0_(db, 'Rental').length);
    var v = qtView_(db, Q1);
    eq('T26 view: modes + added', v.clauses.filter(function (c) { return c.mode !== 'std'; }).map(function (c) { return c.no + ' ' + c.mode; }).join(', ') + ' | ' + v.added.map(function (a) { return a.no + '@' + a.sec; }).join(', '), '3.3 edit, 7.1 remove | 4.5@4, 8.1@8');
    eq('T27 view: custom values shown, rest blank', v.fields.map(function (f) { return f.value === '' ? '-' : f.value; }).join(' '), '30 30 15 3 2 14 Pro rata');
  });
  step('T billing + invoices', function () {
    save(Q4, { settings: { ValidityDays: '60' } });
    save(Q3, { settings: { NoticeDays: '14' } });
    var job = quoteToJob(db, Q1, { date: '22-Nov-2026' });
    throws('T28 terms locked once the job exists', function () { save(Q1, {}); }, /already has job/);
    eq('T29 locked view', qtView_(db, Q1).locked, true);
    postOpeningStock(db, [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 100 }, { ItemCode: 'BD-ST-207', Qty: 40 }], '01-Nov-2026');
    postChallan(db, createChallan(db, { jobNo: job, date: '01-Dec-2026', lines: [{ itemCode: 'RL-STD-20', qty: 50 }, { itemCode: 'BD-ST-207', qty: 20 }] }));
    var i1 = runHireBilling(db, '01-Dec-2026').invoices[0].no;
    postChallan(db, createChallan(db, { jobNo: job, date: '15-Dec-2026', lines: [{ itemCode: 'RL-STD-20', qty: 10 }] }));
    var i2 = runHireBilling(db, '31-Dec-2026').invoices[0];
    eq('T30 quote Pro rata used (Settings say Full period): 160 pc-days', (find(i2.lines.filter(function (l) { return l.ItemCode === 'RL-STD-20'; }), 'Unit', 'day') || {}).Qty, 160);
    var it = JSON.stringify(PDF_SPECS.Invoice(db, i1).blocks);
    eq('T31 invoice PDF: 30 days, 3%, due 31 Dec', /Payable within 30 days/.test(it) && /interest at 3% per month/.test(it) && /31 December 2026/.test(it), true);
    db.update('Invoices', must(db, 'Invoices', 'InvNo', i1), { Status: 'Sent' });
    refreshStatuses(db, '20-Dec-2026');
    eq('T32 not overdue at day 19 (customer standard 7 would be)', must(db, 'Invoices', 'InvNo', i1).Status, 'Sent');
    refreshStatuses(db, '05-Jan-2027');
    eq('T33 overdue after 30 days', must(db, 'Invoices', 'InvNo', i1).Status, 'Overdue');
    eq('T34 expiry per quote (002 standard 30 days expired, 004 60 days not)', q(Q2).Status + ' ' + q(Q4).Status, 'Expired Sent');
    var pj = quoteToJob(db, Q3, { date: '22-Nov-2026' });
    db.insert('OffHire', { OHNNo: 'CAE/OHN/2027/001', JobNo: pj, NoticeDate: toDate('10-Jan-2027'), OffHireDate: toDate('12-Jan-2027'), Scope: 'Full' });
    eq('T35 project hire end = notice + 14 days (quote)', fmtDay(hireEndDay(db, must(db, 'Jobs', 'JobNo', pj))), '24-Jan-2027');
  });
  step('T reset + panel', function () {
    db.update('Quotes', q(Q4), { Status: 'Sent' });
    save(Q4, { added: [{ section: '6', text: 'Extra visit on request.' }] });
    eq('T36 extra clause on a Service quote', qtRows_(db, Q4).length + ' ' + qtRows_(db, Q4)[0].ClauseNo, '1 6.11');
    save(Q4, {});
    eq('T37 reset to standard', qtRows_(db, Q4).length + ' ' + (q(Q4).ValidityDays || '-') + ' ' + qtSummary_(db, q(Q4)), '0 - standard · 7 to fill');
    eq('T38 quote view carries the terms summary', uiGetDb_(db, 'quote', Q2).termsInfo, 'standard · 5 to fill');
    var js = UI_HTML_.slice(UI_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, UI_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('T39 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    eq('T40 Terms screen + button + save flow in the panel', /S\.terms=/.test(UI_HTML_) && /go\('terms',x\.ref,true\)/.test(UI_HTML_) && /btn\('Terms'/.test(UI_HTML_), true);
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_QuoteForm.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — quote form: customer + project picker (add-on QF1) · 15 Nov 2026
 * Twelfth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_QuoteForm › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Code, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq, Site, Roles, Terms, QuoteForm. No menu change.
 *
 * Quote form (New quote / Edit):
 *   Customer — dropdown, or "+ New customer" inline (name needed; address, contact person, designation, phone, email,
 *     BIN, payment days optional). Picked customer: details shown, "Edit details" changes them with the quote.
 *   Project  — 1. shortlist = this customer's projects (exactly one → preselected on a new quote) · 2. search all
 *     projects (2+ letters, name or address, every client): another client's project → a linked Sites row for this
 *     customer (same Project + Address, own SiteID + stock location) · 3. "+ New project" inline (name needed; address,
 *     site contact, phone, start date optional). Picked project: "Edit details".
 *   One click = one lock: customer → project → quote, everything checked before anything is written.
 *   Attention defaults from the contact person (+ designation) when blank.
 * Customers & projects screen (home tile + menu "New customer / site"): find → edit every field, the projects
 *   (edit, add) and the customer's quotes. Replaces typing in the Customers / Sites tabs.
 * Set-up: automatic on the first save (Customers.Designation, and Sites.StartDate if missing, at the END of the tabs).
 * New: actions saveQuoteFull, saveCustomerFull, saveSiteFull · lookups uiCustomerGet(id), uiSiteSearch(q, custId).
 * Wraps: uiInitDb_ (customer + project details for the panel), selfTestAllParts_. Panel: 5 injections into UI_HTML_
 *   (picker in quoteForm, save → saveQuoteFull, home tile, screen code); S.customer replaced by the new screen.
 */
var CAE_QF_VERSION = 'QF1 · 15 Nov 2026';
// [panel key, column]
var QF_CF_ = [['name', 'Name'], ['address', 'Address'], ['contact', 'ContactPerson'], ['designation', 'Designation'], ['phone', 'Phone'], ['email', 'Email'], ['bin', 'BIN'], ['paymentDays', 'PaymentDays']];
var QF_SF_ = [['project', 'Project'], ['address', 'Address'], ['siteRep', 'SiteRep'], ['siteRepPhone', 'SiteRepPhone'], ['startDate', 'StartDate']];

// ======================================================================= set-up (automatic, safe to repeat)
function qfReady_(db) { return db.headers('Customers').indexOf('Designation') >= 0 && db.headers('Sites').indexOf('StartDate') >= 0; }
function upgradeQF(db) {
  var done = [];
  if (db.ensureColumn('Customers', 'Designation')) done.push('Customers + Designation');
  if (db.ensureColumn('Sites', 'StartDate')) {
    done.push('Sites + StartDate');
    if (db.ss) db.sheet('Sites').getRange(2, db.headers('Sites').indexOf('StartDate') + 1, Math.max(db.sheet('Sites').getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1).setNumberFormat('dd-mmm-yyyy');
  }
  if (done.length) log_(db, 'Upgrade QF', '', done.join('; '));
  return done;
}
function qfReadyUp_(db) { if (!qfReady_(db)) upgradeQF(db); }

// ======================================================================= field checks
function qfStr_(v) { return blank(v) ? '' : String(v).trim(); }
function qfKey_(v) { return qfStr_(v).toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' '); }
function qfEmail_(v) {
  var a = qfStr_(v).split(/[,;]/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(function (x) { return x; });
  a.forEach(function (e) { if (!/^[^\s@,;<>()]+@[^\s@,;<>()]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$/.test(e)) throw new Error('Email "' + e + '" is not a valid address'); });
  return a.join(', ');
}
function qfPhone_(v, what) {
  var s = qfStr_(v);
  if (s && (!/\d/.test(s) || /[^\d\s+\-\/,().]/.test(s))) throw new Error(what + ' "' + s + '": use digits, spaces and + - / , ( ) only');
  return s; // text: the sheet keeps leading 0 / + (cellVal_)
}
function qfVal_(db, col, v) {
  if (col === 'Email') return qfEmail_(v);
  if (col === 'Phone') return qfPhone_(v, 'Phone');
  if (col === 'SiteRepPhone') return qfPhone_(v, 'Site contact phone');
  if (col === 'PaymentDays') {
    var s = qfStr_(v); if (!s) return num(S(db, 'PaymentDaysDefault', 7));
    var n = Number(s); if (!(n >= 0 && n <= 365) || Math.round(n) !== n) throw new Error('Payment days must be a whole number from 0 to 365');
    return n;
  }
  if (col === 'StartDate') return qfStr_(v) ? uiDate_(qfStr_(v)) : '';
  return qfStr_(v);
}
function qfSame_(col, a, b) {
  if (col === 'StartDate') return (blank(a) ? null : dayNum(a)) === (blank(b) ? null : dayNum(b));
  if (col === 'PaymentDays') return !blank(a) && !blank(b) && num(a) === num(b);
  return qfStr_(a) === qfStr_(b);
}
// f = panel fields → { column: value }; for an edit (old row) only the columns that change
function qfPatch_(db, defs, f, old, need, what) {
  var p = {};
  defs.forEach(function (d) {
    if (f[d[0]] === undefined) return;
    var v = qfVal_(db, d[1], f[d[0]]);
    if (old && qfSame_(d[1], old[d[1]], v)) return;
    p[d[1]] = v;
  });
  if (need && !old && blank(p[need])) throw new Error(what + ' name is needed');
  if (need && old && (need in p) && p[need] === '') throw new Error(what + ' name cannot be empty');
  return p;
}
function qfDupCust_(db, name, except) {
  var k = qfKey_(name);
  return db.rows('Customers').filter(function (c) { return !blank(c.CustID) && c.CustID !== except && qfKey_(c.Name) === k; })[0] || null;
}
function qfDupSite_(db, cust, project, address, except) {
  return db.rows('Sites').filter(function (s) { return !blank(s.SiteID) && s.SiteID !== except && s.CustID === cust && qfKey_(s.Project) === qfKey_(project) && qfKey_(s.Address) === qfKey_(address); })[0] || null;
}
function qfDupSiteErr_(db, cust, d) { return new Error(((find(db.rows('Customers'), 'CustID', cust) || {}).Name || cust) + ' already has this project (' + d.SiteID + ' · ' + d.Project + ') — pick it from the list'); }

// ======================================================================= plans (checks only) + writes
// c = { mode: new | existing, id, fields } → { row (null = new), patch }
function qfCustPlan_(db, c, fallbackId) {
  c = c || {};
  if (c.mode === 'new') {
    var p = qfPatch_(db, QF_CF_, c.fields || {}, null, 'Name', 'Customer'), d = qfDupCust_(db, p.Name);
    if (d) throw new Error('A customer named "' + d.Name + '" already exists (' + d.CustID + ') — pick it from the list');
    return { row: null, patch: p };
  }
  var id = c.id || fallbackId;
  if (blank(id)) throw new Error('Choose the customer or add a new one');
  var row = must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', id), p2 = c.fields ? qfPatch_(db, QF_CF_, c.fields, row, 'Name', 'Customer') : {};
  if (p2.Name) { var d2 = qfDupCust_(db, p2.Name, id); if (d2) throw new Error('A customer named "' + d2.Name + '" already exists (' + d2.CustID + ')'); }
  return { row: row, patch: p2 };
}
// s = { mode: none | existing | link | new, id, src, fields }
function qfSitePlan_(db, s, cp) {
  s = s || {};
  var mode = s.mode || (blank(s.id) ? 'none' : 'existing'), cid = cp.row ? cp.row.CustID : '';
  if (mode === 'none') return { mode: 'none' };
  if (mode === 'existing') {
    var row = must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', s.id);
    if (row.CustID !== cid) throw new Error('Project ' + s.id + ' belongs to another customer — find it with Search to add it for this customer');
    var p = s.fields ? qfPatch_(db, QF_SF_, s.fields, row, 'Project', 'Project') : {};
    if (('Project' in p) || ('Address' in p)) {
      var d = qfDupSite_(db, cid, 'Project' in p ? p.Project : row.Project, 'Address' in p ? p.Address : row.Address, row.SiteID);
      if (d) throw qfDupSiteErr_(db, cid, d);
    }
    return { mode: mode, row: row, patch: p };
  }
  if (mode === 'link') {
    var src = must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', s.src);
    if (src.CustID === cid) return qfSitePlan_(db, { mode: 'existing', id: src.SiteID }, cp);
    var f = Object.assign({}, s.fields || {}); delete f.project; delete f.address;
    var p2 = qfPatch_(db, QF_SF_, f, null, null);
    p2.Project = qfStr_(src.Project); p2.Address = qfStr_(src.Address);
    var d2 = cid ? qfDupSite_(db, cid, p2.Project, p2.Address) : null;
    if (d2) throw qfDupSiteErr_(db, cid, d2);
    return { mode: mode, src: src, patch: p2 };
  }
  if (mode === 'new') {
    var p3 = qfPatch_(db, QF_SF_, s.fields || {}, null, 'Project', 'Project'), d3 = cid ? qfDupSite_(db, cid, p3.Project, p3.Address) : null;
    if (d3) throw qfDupSiteErr_(db, cid, d3);
    return { mode: mode, patch: p3 };
  }
  throw new Error('Unknown project choice ' + mode);
}
function qfCustWrite_(db, cp) {
  if (cp.row) {
    var keys = Object.keys(cp.patch);
    if (keys.length) { uiPutRow_(db, 'Customers', must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', cp.row.CustID), cp.patch); log_(db, 'Customer updated', cp.row.CustID, keys.join(', ')); }
    return { id: cp.row.CustID, changed: keys };
  }
  var id = nextId(db, 'CUST'), r = Object.assign({ CustID: id, Status: 'Active', PaymentDays: num(S(db, 'PaymentDaysDefault', 7)) }, cp.patch);
  db.insert('Customers', r);
  log_(db, 'Customer added', id, r.Name);
  return { id: id, added: true, changed: [] };
}
function qfSiteWrite_(db, sp, custId) {
  if (sp.mode === 'none') return { id: '', changed: [] };
  if (sp.row) {
    var keys = Object.keys(sp.patch);
    if (keys.length) { uiPutRow_(db, 'Sites', must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', sp.row.SiteID), sp.patch); log_(db, 'Site updated', sp.row.SiteID, keys.join(', ')); }
    return { id: sp.row.SiteID, changed: keys };
  }
  var id = nextId(db, 'SITE'), r = Object.assign({ SiteID: id, CustID: custId, Status: 'Planned' }, sp.patch);
  db.insert('Sites', r);
  ensureSiteLoc(db, r);
  log_(db, sp.mode === 'link' ? 'Site linked' : 'Site added', id, custId + ' · ' + r.Project + (sp.src ? ' (same project as ' + sp.src.SiteID + ' of ' + sp.src.CustID + ')' : ''));
  return { id: id, added: sp.mode, changed: [] };
}
// the checks uiSaveQuote_ makes, without writing (so a bad line stops the new customer / project too)
function qfQuoteCheck_(db, a) {
  var h = a.header || {}, type = h.QuoteType;
  if (UI_LISTS_DEFAULT_.QuoteType.indexOf(type) < 0) throw new Error('Choose the quote type');
  uiQuoteLines_(db, a.lines, type); uiDate_(h.QuoteDate); uiStaff_(db, h.PreparedBy);
  if (!a.quoteNo) return;
  var no = a.quoteNo, q = must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', no), job = find(db.rows('Jobs'), 'QuoteNo', no);
  if (job) throw new Error(no + ' already has job ' + job.JobNo + ' — change it by a variation on the job');
  if (['Draft', 'Sent'].indexOf(q.Status) < 0) throw new Error(no + ' is ' + q.Status + ' — use Revise to reopen it');
  if (milestoneRows(db, no).some(function (m) { return m.Status !== 'Pending'; })) throw new Error(no + ': a milestone is already reached');
}
// a = { quoteNo, header, lines, customer: { mode, id, fields }, site: { mode, id, src, fields } }
function qfSaveQuoteFull_(db, a) {
  var h = Object.assign({}, a.header || {}), cp = qfCustPlan_(db, a.customer, h.CustID);
  var sp = qfSitePlan_(db, a.site || { mode: blank(h.SiteID) ? 'none' : 'existing', id: h.SiteID }, cp);
  qfQuoteCheck_(db, { quoteNo: a.quoteNo, header: h, lines: a.lines });
  // everything checked — now write: customer, project, quote
  qfReadyUp_(db);
  var cw = qfCustWrite_(db, cp), sw = qfSiteWrite_(db, sp, cw.id), cu = must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', cw.id);
  h.CustID = cw.id; h.SiteID = sw.id;
  if (blank(h.Attention) && !blank(cu.ContactPerson)) h.Attention = qfStr_(cu.ContactPerson) + (blank(cu.Designation) ? '' : ', ' + qfStr_(cu.Designation));
  var r = uiSaveQuote_(db, { quoteNo: a.quoteNo, header: h, lines: a.lines }), pre = [];
  if (cw.added) pre.push('New customer ' + cw.id + '.'); else if (cw.changed.length) pre.push(cw.id + ' details updated (' + cw.changed.join(', ') + ').');
  if (sw.added) pre.push((sw.added === 'link' ? 'Shared project added for this customer: ' : 'New project ') + sw.id + '.'); else if (sw.changed.length) pre.push(sw.id + ' details updated (' + sw.changed.join(', ') + ').');
  r.msg = (pre.length ? pre.join(' ') + '\n' : '') + r.msg; r.cust = cw.id; r.site = sw.id;
  return r;
}
function qfSaveCustomer_(db, a) {
  var cp = qfCustPlan_(db, a.id ? { mode: 'existing', id: a.id, fields: a.fields || {} } : { mode: 'new', fields: a.fields || {} });
  qfReadyUp_(db);
  var w = qfCustWrite_(db, cp);
  return { msg: w.added ? 'Customer ' + w.id + ' added.' : w.changed.length ? w.id + ' saved: ' + w.changed.join(', ') + '.' : 'No changes.', ref: w.id };
}
function qfSaveSite_(db, a) {
  var row = a.id ? must(db, 'Sites', 'SiteID', a.id) : null, cp = { row: must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', row ? row.CustID : a.custId), patch: {} };
  var sp = qfSitePlan_(db, row ? { mode: 'existing', id: a.id, fields: a.fields || {} } : { mode: 'new', fields: a.fields || {} }, cp);
  qfReadyUp_(db);
  var w = qfSiteWrite_(db, sp, cp.row.CustID);
  return { msg: w.added ? 'Project ' + w.id + ' added.' : w.changed.length ? w.id + ' saved: ' + w.changed.join(', ') + '.' : 'No changes.', ref: w.id };
}
UI_ACTIONS_.saveQuoteFull = function (db, a) { return qfSaveQuoteFull_(db, a); };
UI_ACTIONS_.saveCustomerFull = function (db, a) { return qfSaveCustomer_(db, a); };
UI_ACTIONS_.saveSiteFull = function (db, a) { return qfSaveSite_(db, a); };

// ======================================================================= lookups (read-only)
function qfCustOut_(c) {
  return { id: c.CustID, name: c.Name, status: c.Status || '', address: qfStr_(c.Address), contact: qfStr_(c.ContactPerson), designation: qfStr_(c.Designation), phone: qfStr_(c.Phone),
    email: qfStr_(c.Email), bin: qfStr_(c.BIN), paymentDays: blank(c.PaymentDays) ? '' : num(c.PaymentDays) };
}
function qfSiteOut_(s) {
  return { id: s.SiteID, cust: s.CustID, project: qfStr_(s.Project), address: qfStr_(s.Address), siteRep: qfStr_(s.SiteRep), siteRepPhone: qfStr_(s.SiteRepPhone),
    startDate: blank(s.StartDate) ? '' : toDate(s.StartDate), status: s.Status || '', loc: s.LocID || '' };
}
function qfCustomerView_(db, id) {
  var c = must(db, 'Customers', 'CustID', id), names = {}, sites = db.rows('Sites').filter(function (s) { return !blank(s.SiteID); });
  db.rows('Customers').forEach(function (x) { names[x.CustID] = x.Name; });
  return {
    cust: qfCustOut_(c),
    projects: sites.filter(function (s) { return s.CustID === id; }).map(function (s) {
      var o = qfSiteOut_(s);
      o.shared = sites.filter(function (x) { return x.CustID !== id && qfKey_(x.Project) === qfKey_(s.Project) && qfKey_(x.Address) === qfKey_(s.Address); }).map(function (x) { return names[x.CustID] || x.CustID; });
      return o;
    }),
    quotes: db.rows('Quotes').filter(function (q) { return q.CustID === id && !blank(q.QuoteNo); }).map(function (q) {
      var s = find(sites, 'SiteID', q.SiteID);
      return { no: q.QuoteNo, rev: num(q.Rev), type: q.QuoteType, date: q.QuoteDate, status: q.Status, site: s ? s.Project : '', total: quoteTotals(db, q.QuoteNo).total };
    }).reverse(),
  };
}
// 2+ letters, project name or address, every client; this customer's own projects first
function qfSiteSearch_(db, q, custId) {
  var k = qfKey_(q), names = {};
  if (k.length < 2) return [];
  db.rows('Customers').forEach(function (x) { names[x.CustID] = x.Name; });
  return db.rows('Sites').filter(function (s) { return !blank(s.SiteID) && (qfKey_(s.Project) + ' ' + qfKey_(s.Address)).indexOf(k) >= 0; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.CustID === custId ? 0 : 1) - (b.CustID === custId ? 0 : 1); }).slice(0, 20)
    .map(function (s) { var o = qfSiteOut_(s); o.custName = names[s.CustID] || s.CustID; return o; });
}
function uiCustomerGet(id) { return uiRun_(function (db) { return qfCustomerView_(db, id); }, true); }
function uiSiteSearch(q, custId) { return uiRun_(function (db) { return qfSiteSearch_(db, q, custId); }, true); }
var QF_init0_ = uiInitDb_;
uiInitDb_ = function (db) {
  var r = QF_init0_(db), cu = {}, si = {};
  db.rows('Customers').forEach(function (c) { cu[c.CustID] = c; });
  db.rows('Sites').forEach(function (s) { si[s.SiteID] = s; });
  r.customers.forEach(function (c) { if (cu[c.id]) Object.assign(c, qfCustOut_(cu[c.id])); });
  r.sites.forEach(function (s) { if (si[s.id]) Object.assign(s, qfSiteOut_(si[s.id])); });
  return r;
};

        el('b',{text:s.project||s.id}),el('span',{text:[s.address,custName(s.cust)+(own||dup?' (this customer)':'')].filter(function(x){return x;}).join(' · ')})));});};
  cSel.onchange=function(){cChg(true);};pSel.onchange=function(){st.pe=false;drawP();};
  var box=el('div',{style:'grid-column:1/-1'},el('div',{cls:'g'},cPick),newC,cArea,el('div',{cls:'g'},pRow,fld('…or search all projects',q)),res,newP,pArea);
  cChg(isNew);
  return {box:box,get:function(){var c={mode:st.cm==='new'?'new':'existing',id:cid()};if(cf)c.fields=cf.get();
    var s={mode:st.pm==='new'?'new':st.pm==='link'?'link':(pSel.value?'existing':'none'),id:st.pm==='pick'?pSel.value:'',src:st.pm==='link'?st.src:''};if(pf)s.fields=pf.get();
    return {customer:c,site:s};}};}
function qfCustScreen(id){var f=inp('','text','name, contact, phone or email'),list=el('div'),box=el('div');
  function draw(){list.innerHTML='';var t=qfLow(f.value),rows=D.customers.filter(function(c){return !t||qfLow([c.name,c.contact,c.phone,c.email,c.id].join(' ')).indexOf(t)>=0;});

function selfTestQF() {

@@@@FILE CAE_Ops_QuoteForm2.gs@@@@
/**
 * Core Access Ops — quote form 2: one "search · pick · add new" box + measured lines (QF1b) + "where am I" banner (QF2) · 15 Nov 2026
 * Thirteenth script file, LAST: Apps Script › + › Script › name it CAE_Ops_QuoteForm2 › paste › Save › reload the sheet.
 * File order: Code, PDF, UI, Reports, App, Sets, Order, QuoteReq, Site, Roles, Terms, QuoteForm, QuoteForm2. No menu change.
 *
 * Customer + project: ONE combobox each (qfCombo). Type to filter (name, contact, phone, email, address / project,
 *   address, client); pick = select; the last row is always "+ Add new …: '<typed text>'" → inline new form, name filled.
 *   Project list: this customer's projects first, then other clients' (marked "will be shared" → linked Sites row).
 *   Replaces QF1's qfPicker in the panel (a later declaration of the same name); server API unchanged (saveQuoteFull).
 * Quote types: "Service" is hidden for NEW quotes (= Project, decided 15 Nov). Old Service quotes keep it and work;
 *   nothing renamed in the sheet. Server refuses a new Service quote / a change to Service.
 * Measured lines: Measure (No. / L × H m² / L × W × H m³) × No. of sets → Qty + Unit (worked out again on the server),
 *   Rate basis (Per unit / Lump sum = 1 lot), Billing default Monthly for Project / Rental (One-off allowed), One-off for Sale.
 *   QuoteLines + DimL, DimW, DimH, DimN, RateBasis at the END (automatic on the first save). Quote PDF: the
 *   description gets "L 20 m × H 12 m = 240 m²". Amount = Qty × Months × Rate as before — totals / invoices unchanged.
 * QF2 banner: under the black CORE ACCESS header a full-width teal bar (#17707D, white caps) with the screen name and,
 *   on the right, the open record (e.g. "CAE/Q/2026/003 · Rev 1 · Draft", "CAE/J/… · Project · On hire"). Set in go()
 *   and refined when the record has loaded (quoteView, quoteForm, jobView, termsView). Sidebar / dialog title
 *   "Core Access · <screen>" (screen it was opened on). O7 / QR / IE panels unchanged (own small panels).
 * 15 Nov 2026 19:50: customer + project boxes show a ▾ arrow and a "+ New" button (easier to find).
 * Replaces by name: uiOpen_, qrOpenQuote (title only).
 * Wraps: qfReady_, upgradeQF, uiQuoteLines_, UI_ACTIONS_.saveQuoteFull, PDF_SPECS.Quote, selfTestAllParts_.
 *   Panel: 5 injections into UI_HTML_ (QF1b code, type dropdown, line hook, home tile; QF2 banner code).
 */
var CAE_QF1B_VERSION = 'QF1b · 15 Nov 2026';
var CAE_QF2_VERSION = 'QF2 · 15 Nov 2026';
var QF_DIM_COLS_ = ['DimL', 'DimW', 'DimH', 'DimN', 'RateBasis'];

// ======================================================================= set-up (automatic with QF1's, safe to repeat)
function qfDimReady_(db) { var h = db.headers('QuoteLines'); return QF_DIM_COLS_.every(function (c) { return h.indexOf(c) >= 0; }); }
var QF1B_ready0_ = qfReady_;
qfReady_ = function (db) { return QF1B_ready0_(db) && qfDimReady_(db); };
var QF1B_up0_ = upgradeQF;
upgradeQF = function (db) {
  var done = QF1B_up0_(db), add = [];
  QF_DIM_COLS_.forEach(function (c) { if (db.ensureColumn('QuoteLines', c)) add.push(c); });
  if (add.length) { done.push('QuoteLines + ' + add.join(', ')); log_(db, 'Upgrade QF1b', '', add.join(', ')); }
  // a new column copies the Yes/No list from Selected (insertColumnAfter) — clear it, or saving a size fails
  if (add.length && typeof SheetDb === 'function' && db instanceof SheetDb) { var qs = db.sheet('QuoteLines'), qh = db.headers('QuoteLines'); add.forEach(function (c) { qs.getRange(1, qh.indexOf(c) + 1, qs.getMaxRows(), 1).clearDataValidations(); }); }
  return done;
};

// ======================================================================= measured lines
function qfNumTxt_(v) { return String(Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000); }
// l = line from the panel → null (no measure) or { qty (null = typed), unit, basis, L, W, H, N, text }
function qfDim_(l, i) {
  var n = 'Line ' + (i + 1) + ': ', basis = qfStr_(l.RateBasis);
  if (basis && ['Per unit', 'Lump sum'].indexOf(basis) < 0) throw new Error(n + 'Rate basis must be Per unit or Lump sum');
  var dn = function (k, what) { var s = qfStr_(l[k]); if (!s) return ''; var v = Number(s); if (!(v > 0)) throw new Error(n + what + ' must be a number above 0'); return v; };
  var L = dn('DimL', 'length'), W = dn('DimW', 'width'), H = dn('DimH', 'height'), N = dn('DimN', 'no. of sets');
  if (N !== '' && Math.round(N) !== N) throw new Error(n + 'no. of sets must be a whole number');
  if (basis === 'Lump sum') return { qty: 1, unit: 'lot', basis: basis, L: '', W: '', H: '', N: '', text: '' };
  if (L === '' && W === '' && H === '' && N === '') return basis ? { qty: null, basis: basis, L: '', W: '', H: '', N: '', text: '' } : null;
  if (L === '' || H === '') throw new Error(n + 'give length and height (m²), or length, width and height (m³)');
  var k = N === '' ? 1 : N, q = Math.round(L * (W === '' ? 1 : W) * H * k * 1000) / 1000, u = W === '' ? 'm²' : 'm³';
  var text = 'L ' + qfNumTxt_(L) + ' m × ' + (W !== '' ? 'W ' + qfNumTxt_(W) + ' m × ' : '') + 'H ' + qfNumTxt_(H) + ' m' + (k > 1 ? ' × ' + k + ' sets' : '') + ' = ' + qfNumTxt_(q) + ' ' + u;
  return { qty: q, unit: u, basis: basis || 'Per unit', L: L, W: W, H: H, N: N, text: text };
}
function qfDimTxt_(l) { try { var d = qfDim_(l, 0); return d && d.text ? d.text : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
var QF1B_lines0_ = uiQuoteLines_;
uiQuoteLines_ = function (db, lines, type) {
  if (!lines || !lines.length) return QF1B_lines0_(db, lines, type);
  var pre = lines.map(function (l, i) {
    var d = qfDim_(l, i), o = Object.assign({}, l);
    if (d && d.qty != null) { o.Qty = d.qty; o.Unit = d.unit; }
    return { o: o, d: d };
  });
  var out = QF1B_lines0_(db, pre.map(function (x) { return x.o; }), type);
  if (qfDimReady_(db)) out.forEach(function (r, i) {
    var d = pre[i].d || {};
    r.DimL = d.L || ''; r.DimW = d.W || ''; r.DimH = d.H || ''; r.DimN = d.N || ''; r.RateBasis = d.basis || '';
  });
  return out;
};
// Quote PDF: measurement under the description
var QF1B_specQ0_ = PDF_SPECS.Quote;
PDF_SPECS.Quote = function (db, no) {
  var spec = QF1B_specQ0_(db, no);
  if (!qfDimReady_(db)) return spec;
  var lines = db.rows('QuoteLines').filter(function (l) { return String(l.QuoteNo) === String(no) && (l.Selected === 'Yes' || l.Optional === 'Yes'); })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return num(a.LineNo) - num(b.LineNo); });
  var g = spec.blocks.filter(function (b) { return b.t === 'grid'; })[0];
  if (g) lines.forEach(function (l, i) { var t = qfDimTxt_(l); if (t && g.rows[i]) g.rows[i][1] = g.rows[i][1] + '\n' + t; });
  return spec;
};

// ======================================================================= quote type: Service = Project for new quotes
function qfTypeGuard_(db, a) {
  if ((a.header || {}).QuoteType !== 'Service') return;
  var old = a.quoteNo ? find(db.rows('Quotes'), 'QuoteNo', a.quoteNo) : null;
  if (!old || old.QuoteType !== 'Service') throw new Error('Service quotes are now Project quotes — choose Project');
}
var QF1B_sqf0_ = UI_ACTIONS_.saveQuoteFull;
UI_ACTIONS_.saveQuoteFull = function (db, a) { qfTypeGuard_(db, a); return QF1B_sqf0_(db, a); };

// ======================================================================= combobox filter (same code runs in the panel)
// rows filtered by every typed word (any field); own rows first; last entry = { add: typed text }
function qfComboList_(rows, q, fields, own, max) {
  var t = String(q == null ? '' : q).trim(), w = t.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(function (x) { return x; });
  var m = rows.filter(function (r) {
    var hay = fields.map(function (f) { return r[f] == null ? '' : String(r[f]); }).join(' ').toLowerCase();
    return w.every(function (x) { return hay.indexOf(x) >= 0; });
  });
  if (own) m = m.filter(own).concat(m.filter(function (r) { return !own(r); }));
  return m.slice(0, max || 12).map(function (r) { return { row: r }; }).concat([{ add: t }]);
}

function qfTypes(cur){return D.lists.QuoteType.filter(function(t){return t!=='Service'||cur==='Service';});}
// o = { rows(), fields, own(r), label(r) → [main, sub], onPick(r|null), onAdd(text), ph, addWord }
function qfCombo(o){var x=inp('','text',o.ph),list=el('div',{cls:'qfl'}),cur=null;
  function set(r){cur=r||null;x.value=cur?o.label(cur)[0]:'';}
  function show(){list.innerHTML='';
    qfComboList_(o.rows(),cur?'':x.value,o.fields,o.own,12).forEach(function(it){var d;
      if(it.add!=null)d=el('div',{cls:'qfi qfa',text:'+ Add new '+o.addWord+(it.add?': “'+it.add+'”':'')});
      else{var lb=o.label(it.row);d=el('div',{cls:'qfi'+(cur&&cur.id===it.row.id?' on':'')},el('b',{text:lb[0]}),lb[1]?el('span',{text:lb[1]}):null);}
      d.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){e.preventDefault();list.style.display='none';
        if(it.add!=null){var t=it.add;set(null);o.onAdd(t);}else{set(it.row);o.onPick(it.row);}});
      add(list,d);});
    list.style.display='block';}
  x.onfocus=show;
  x.oninput=function(){if(cur){cur=null;o.onPick(null);}show();};
  x.onblur=function(){setTimeout(function(){list.style.display='none';if(!cur)x.value='';},150);};
  x.onkeydown=function(e){if(e.key==='Escape')list.style.display='none';else if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();if(list.firstChild)list.firstChild.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown'));}};
  var nb=btn('+ New',function(){},'s x');nb.title='Add a new '+o.addWord;
  nb.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){e.preventDefault();var t=cur?'':x.value.trim();list.style.display='none';set(null);o.onAdd(t);});
  return {box:el('div',{cls:'qfc'},el('div',{cls:'qfr'},el('div',{cls:'qfw'},x),nb),list),set:set,input:x};}

    onAdd:function(t){st.pm='new';st.pe=false;drawP({project:t});}});
  function att(){var c=st.cm==='new'||st.ce?(cf?cf.get():{}):(qfCust(st.cid)||{}),a=c.contact?c.contact+(c.designation?', '+c.designation:''):'';

      add(cArea,qfCard('New customer — only the name is needed now',['Cancel',function(){st.cm='pick';st.cid='';cc.set(null);pReset(false);drawC();}],[cf.box,w]));att();return;}

    return {customer:c,site:s};}};}
// measured line: added to every quote line card (c = line, l = saved line, F = quote fields)
function qfDim(c,l,F,upd){var t=F.QuoteType.value,fresh=!l.LineID&&!l.Description&&!l.ItemCode;
  var M=sel([['','No. — type the qty'],['A','Area: L × H (m²)'],['V','Volume: L × W × H (m³)']],l.DimW?'V':l.DimL?'A':(fresh&&t==='Project'?'A':''));
  var RB=sel(['Per unit','Lump sum'],l.RateBasis==='Lump sum'?'Lump sum':'Per unit'),dL=inp(l.DimL,'number','m'),dW=inp(l.DimW,'number','m'),dH=inp(l.DimH,'number','m'),dN=inp(l.DimN,'number','1'),txt=el('p',{cls:'muted'});
  var fW=fld('Width W (m)',dW),dims=el('div',{cls:'g'},fld('Length L (m)',dL),fW,fld('Height H (m)',dH),fld('No. of sets',dN));
  var box=el('div',null,el('div',{cls:'g'},fld('Measure',M),fld('Rate basis',RB)),dims,txt);
  function calc(){var ls=RB.value==='Lump sum',m=ls?'':M.value;M.disabled=ls;dims.style.display=m?'':'none';fW.style.display=m==='V'?'':'none';txt.textContent='';
    if(ls){c.qt.value=1;c.un.value='lot';c.qt.disabled=true;txt.textContent='Lump sum: 1 lot, amount = rate.';}
    else if(m){var L=+dL.value||0,W=m==='V'?(+dW.value||0):1,H=+dH.value||0,N=dN.value===''?1:(+dN.value||0),q=Math.round(L*W*H*N*1000)/1000;
      c.qt.value=q||'';c.qt.disabled=true;c.un.value=m==='A'?'m²':'m³';
      txt.textContent=q?'L '+L+' m × '+(m==='V'?'W '+W+' m × ':'')+'H '+H+' m'+(N>1?' × '+N+' sets':'')+' = '+q+' '+c.un.value:'Enter the sizes — Qty is worked out.';}
    else c.qt.disabled=false;upd();}
  [M,RB,c.it].forEach(function(e){e.addEventListener('change',calc);});[dL,dW,dH,dN].forEach(function(e){e.addEventListener('input',calc);});
  if(fresh){c.bi.value=t==='Sale'?'One-off':'Monthly';if(c.bi.value==='Monthly'&&!c.mo.value)c.mo.value=F.HireMonths.value||1;}
  c.bi.addEventListener('change',function(){if(c.bi.value==='One-off')c.mo.value='';else if(c.bi.value==='Monthly'&&!c.mo.value)c.mo.value=F.HireMonths.value||1;upd();});
  c.card.insertBefore(box,c.card.children[2]||null);
  var g0=c.get;c.get=function(){var o=g0(),m=RB.value==='Lump sum'?'':M.value;o.RateBasis=RB.value;o.DimL=m?dL.value:'';o.DimW=m==='V'?dW.value:'';o.DimH=m?dH.value:'';o.DimN=m?dN.value:'';return o;};
  calc();}
}

// ======================================================================= QF2 "where am I" banner (same helpers run in the panel)
// screen key → name (banner shows it in caps); unknown keys: the panel's TITLES, else the key itself
function qf2Screen_(s, titles) {
  var m = { home: 'Home', quote: 'Quote', quotes: 'Quotes', terms: 'Terms', job: 'Job', customer: 'Customers', challan: 'Challan', ret: 'Return', 'return': 'Return',
    post: 'Challans', billing: 'Billing', payment: 'Payment', po: 'PO', receive: 'Receive goods', expense: 'Expense', pdf: 'PDF' };
  var k = String(s == null || s === '' ? 'home' : s);
  return m[k] || String((titles && titles[k]) || k);
}

  else SpreadsheetApp.getUi().showSidebar(out);
};

// ======================================================================= self-test (in memory, your data is not touched)
if (typeof selfTestAllParts_ === 'function') {
  var QF1B_parts0_ = selfTestAllParts_;

function selfTestQF1b() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step, throws = T.throws, db = testSeed_('Full period');
  var run = function (x, a) { return uiDoDb_(db, x, a); };
  var H = function (o) { return Object.assign({ QuoteType: 'Project', QuoteDate: '2026-11-20', VATPct: '15%', AdvancePct: '50%', CustID: 'CUST-0001' }, o || {}); };
  var CU = { mode: 'existing', id: 'CUST-0001' }, NOS = { mode: 'none' };
  var save = function (no, h, lines) { return run('saveQuoteFull', { quoteNo: no || '', header: h, lines: lines, customer: CU, site: NOS }); };
  var ids = function (r) { return r.map(function (x) { return x.add != null ? '+' + x.add : x.row.id; }).join(); };
  step('G panel', function () {
    eq('G1 panel injected (4 places)', QF1B_INJECTED_, 4);
    var js = UI_HTML_.slice(UI_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, UI_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('G2 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    eq('G3 new picker replaces QF1 (declared later)', UI_HTML_.lastIndexOf('function qfPicker(') > UI_HTML_.indexOf('QF1b combobox') && UI_HTML_.indexOf('QF1b combobox') > UI_HTML_.indexOf('function qfPicker('), true);
    eq('G4 type list hides Service for new quotes + line hook', /QuoteType:sel\(qfTypes\(h\.QuoteType\)/.test(UI_HTML_) && /qfDim\(c,l,F,upd\);L\.push\(c\)/.test(UI_HTML_) && UI_HTML_.indexOf("'Sale · Rental · Project · Service'") < 0, true);
    eq('G35 customer + project boxes look like dropdowns (arrow) + New button', UI_HTML_.indexOf('.qfw::after{content:"▾"') > 0 && UI_HTML_.indexOf("nb=btn('+ New'") > 0, true);
  });
  step('G combobox filter + add new', function () {
    var C = [{ id: 'CUST-1', name: 'Delta Builders', contact: 'Karim', phone: '01711' }, { id: 'CUST-2', name: 'Echo Ltd', contact: 'Delta rep', phone: '029' }], F = ['name', 'contact', 'phone', 'id'];
    eq('G5 matches any field, add-new row last with the typed text', ids(qfComboList_(C, ' Delta ', F)), 'CUST-1,CUST-2,+Delta');
    eq('G6 every word must match', ids(qfComboList_(C, 'delta karim', F)), 'CUST-1,+delta karim');
    eq('G7 by phone', ids(qfComboList_(C, '029', F)), 'CUST-2,+029');
    eq('G8 empty = all + plain add', ids(qfComboList_(C, '', F)), 'CUST-1,CUST-2,+');
    eq('G9 nothing found = only add new', ids(qfComboList_(C, 'zzz', F)), '+zzz');
    var Sx = [{ id: 'S1', cust: 'A', project: 'Tower' }, { id: 'S2', cust: 'B', project: 'Tower 2' }, { id: 'S3', cust: 'B', project: 'Depot' }];
    eq('G10 this customer\'s projects first', ids(qfComboList_(Sx, 'tower', ['project'], function (s) { return s.cust === 'B'; })), 'S2,S1,+tower');
  });
  step('G dimensions → qty', function () {
    var d = qfDim_({ DimL: 20, DimH: 12 }, 0);
    eq('G11 m² = L × H', [d.qty, d.unit, d.text].join('|'), '240|m²|L 20 m × H 12 m = 240 m²');
    d = qfDim_({ DimL: '10', DimW: '1.2', DimH: '5' }, 0);
    eq('G12 m³ = L × W × H', [d.qty, d.unit, d.text].join('|'), '60|m³|L 10 m × W 1.2 m × H 5 m = 60 m³');
    d = qfDim_({ DimL: 20, DimH: 12, DimN: 3 }, 0);
    eq('G13 × no. of sets', [d.qty, d.text].join('|'), '720|L 20 m × H 12 m × 3 sets = 720 m²');
    d = qfDim_({ RateBasis: 'Lump sum', DimL: 5 }, 0);
    eq('G14 lump sum = 1 lot', [d.qty, d.unit, d.L].join('|'), '1|lot|');
    eq('G15 no sizes = qty typed', qfDim_({ Qty: 4 }, 0), null);
    throws('G16 length without height', function () { qfDim_({ DimL: 20 }, 1); }, /Line 2: give length and height/);
    throws('G17 sets must be whole', function () { qfDim_({ DimL: 2, DimH: 2, DimN: 1.5 }, 0); }, /whole number/);
    throws('G18 negative size', function () { qfDim_({ DimL: -2, DimH: 2 }, 0); }, /above 0/);
    throws('G19 rate basis list', function () { qfDim_({ RateBasis: 'Per day' }, 0); }, /Per unit or Lump sum/);
  });
  var Q1;
  step('G save measured lines', function () {
    var c0 = db.rows('Quotes').length;
    throws('G20 bad size: nothing saved', function () { save('', H(), [{ Description: 'x', DimL: 3, Rate: 1, Billing: 'One-off' }]); }, /length and height/);
    eq('G21 …no quote, no columns yet', db.rows('Quotes').length + ' ' + qfDimReady_(db), c0 + ' false');
    var r = save('', H(), [
      { Description: 'Scaffold erection', Qty: 1, DimL: '20', DimH: '12', RateBasis: 'Per unit', Billing: 'Monthly', Months: 2, Rate: 50 },
      { Description: 'Mobilisation', Qty: 7, RateBasis: 'Lump sum', Billing: 'One-off', Rate: 15000 },
      { ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 10, Billing: 'One-off', Rate: 90 }]);
    Q1 = r.ref;
    var L = quoteLines(db, Q1);
    eq('G22 qty + unit worked out on the server, sizes stored', [L[0].Qty, L[0].Unit, L[0].DimL, L[0].DimH, L[0].DimW, L[0].RateBasis].join(' '), '240 m² 20 12  Per unit');
    eq('G23 lump sum line', [L[1].Qty, L[1].Unit, L[1].RateBasis, L[1].Months].join(' '), '1 lot Lump sum ');
    eq('G24 totals as before: (240×2×50 + 15000 + 900) × 1.15', quoteTotals(db, Q1).total, 45885);
    eq('G25 new columns at the END of QuoteLines', db.headers('QuoteLines').slice(-5).join(), QF_DIM_COLS_.join());
    var g = PDF_SPECS.Quote(db, Q1).blocks.filter(function (b) { return b.t === 'grid'; })[0];
    eq('G26 PDF: measure under the description', g.rows[0][1], 'Scaffold erection\nL 20 m × H 12 m = 240 m²');
    eq('G27 PDF: line without sizes unchanged', g.rows[2][1], 'Ringlock standard 2.0 m');
    r = save('', H({ QuoteType: 'Rental' }), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 10, Months: 2, Billing: 'Monthly', Rate: 90 }, { Description: 'Transport', Unit: 'trip', Qty: 2, Billing: 'One-off', Rate: 4000 }]);
    eq('G28 one-off line on a Rental quote: (1800 + 8000) × 1.15', quoteTotals(db, r.ref).total, 11270);
    save(Q1, H(), [{ Description: 'Scaffold erection', Unit: 'm²', Qty: 5, Billing: 'Monthly', Months: 2, Rate: 50 }]);
    L = quoteLines(db, Q1);
    eq('G29 edit: sizes cleared, typed qty kept, extra lines gone', [L.length, L[0].Qty, L[0].DimL, L[0].RateBasis].join(' '), '1 5  ');
  });
  step('G Service = Project', function () {
    throws('G30 new Service quote refused', function () { save('', H({ QuoteType: 'Service' }), [{ Description: 'Inspection', Unit: 'visit', Qty: 1, Rate: 5000 }]); }, /now Project/);
    var r = save('', H({ QuoteType: 'Rental' }), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1, Rate: 90 }]);
    throws('G31 changing a quote to Service refused', function () { save(r.ref, H({ QuoteType: 'Service' }), [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 1, Rate: 90 }]); }, /now Project/);
    db.update('Quotes', must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', r.ref), { QuoteType: 'Service' }); // an old Service quote
    eq('G32 old Service quote still saves', / saved: /.test(save(r.ref, H({ QuoteType: 'Service' }), [{ Description: 'Inspection', Unit: 'visit', Qty: 2, Rate: 5000 }]).msg), true);
    eq('G33 …keeps its type, totals and prints', must(db, 'Quotes', 'QuoteNo', r.ref).QuoteType + ' ' + quoteTotals(db, r.ref).total + ' ' + (PDF_SPECS.Quote(db, r.ref).blocks.length > 0), 'Service 11500 true');
    eq('G34 old path (saveQuote) without sizes still works', / created: /.test(run('saveQuote', { header: H({ QuoteType: 'Sale' }), lines: [{ ItemCode: 'RL-STD-20', Qty: 3, Rate: 100 }] }).msg), true);
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}
function selfTestQF2() {
  var T = testKit_(), eq = T.eq, step = T.step;
  step('B banner', function () {
    eq('B1 banner code injected once', QF2_INJECTED_, 1);
    var js = UI_HTML_.slice(UI_HTML_.indexOf('<script>') + 8, UI_HTML_.lastIndexOf('</script>'));
    eq('B2 panel script compiles', (function () { try { new Function(js); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { return e.message; } })(), 'ok');
    var b = UI_HTML_.indexOf('QF2 banner'), st = UI_HTML_.lastIndexOf('// ================= start');
    eq('B3 after QF1b, before start; go() wrapped', b > UI_HTML_.indexOf('QF1b combobox') && b < st && UI_HTML_.indexOf('go=function(s,arg,keep){setBanner(s,') > b, true);
    eq('B4 teal bar under the black header, white caps', /#qfBan\{[^}]*background:#17707D;color:#fff/.test(UI_HTML_) && /#qfBanS\{[^}]*text-transform:uppercase/.test(UI_HTML_) && /header\{background:#000/.test(UI_HTML_), true);
    eq('B5 screen names', ['quote', 'quotes', 'terms', 'job', 'challan', 'ret', 'return', 'billing', 'payment', 'po', 'expense', 'pdf', 'customer', 'home', ''].map(function (s) { return qf2Screen_(s).toUpperCase(); }).join(' · '),
      'QUOTE · QUOTES · TERMS · JOB · CHALLAN · RETURN · RETURN · BILLING · PAYMENT · PO · EXPENSE · PDF · CUSTOMERS · HOME · HOME');
    eq('B6 unknown screen: panel title, else the key', qf2Screen_('stock', { stock: 'Stock count' }) + ' | ' + qf2Screen_('xyz'), 'Stock count | xyz');
    eq('B7 open quote ref', qf2Ref_({ QuoteNo: 'CAE/Q/2026/003', Rev: 1, Status: 'Draft' }) + ' | ' + qf2Ref_({ QuoteNo: 'CAE/Q/2026/001', Rev: '', Status: 'Sent' }) + ' | ' + qf2Ref_({}), 'CAE/Q/2026/003 · Rev 1 · Draft | CAE/Q/2026/001 · Rev 0 · Sent | ');
    eq('B8 sidebar / dialog title', qf2Title_('quotes') + ' | ' + qf2Title_('return') + ' | ' + qf2Title_(), 'Core Access · Quotes | Core Access · Return | Core Access · Home');
    eq('B9 menu opens with the screen title (uiOpen_ replaced)', /qf2Title_\(screen\)/.test(uiOpen_.toString()), true);
    eq('B10 record hooks in the panel', ['quoteView=function', 'quoteForm=function', 'jobView=function', 'termsView=function'].every(function (x) { return UI_HTML_.indexOf(x) > b; }), true);
  });
  return { pass: T.pass, fail: T.fail, log: T.log };
}
